# Development Process And Speed

_how to build fast, scope, MVP, release cadence, workflow, quitting/pivoting._

Evidence base: 1238 extracted items from 567 videos by 1Coal, 1_Bruce, AlvinBlox, ArTheDev, B Ricey, BloxxedDev, BubuTheDev, Business of Creators, ByteBlox, CallMeDogey, Creators on the Blox, Crusherfire, Cumin, Daiorg, Deconstructor of Fun, DitchyDevelopments, Duckable, Emancyphur, Erthlord, GameMakers, GamesBeat, Generalist Programmer, GnomeCode, ItsZyonic, JustDucksey, Last Level Studios, Leif, Lnor Roblox, LovJak, Matt The Method, MonarchDev, MoseyTube, Mulch, PineappleDev, PolarBear, Possible Panda, Quez, Raul, RoDev, Roblox, Roblox Creator Docs, Roblox Creators, Roblox DevForum, Roblox public API, Roox4, RoyalDev, SimTek Game Development, SmartyRBX, StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data), Suphi Kaner, TheDankDeveloper, TheDevKing, TheNexusAvenger, Tizzy, Ventures, VuukStudios, Yann, bubDEV, janisjanis01, kra_DeZ, mats-dept2, rasdev, studio rook., tef, waveshine, {EISABOOS} "RBX" (2016-04-04 to 2026-11-01). Roblox changes quickly: check the date on each source and see Date context below.

## Overview
Roblox's official 2026 material pushes an AI-assisted build loop: plan and interrogate before building, then commit and auto-publish to a phone-playable build in about a minute. It also reports shipping data (130 roadmap features, 79% on time) and a case of rebuilding a live game's foundation after 14 months. Indie sources add little here beyond cloud version control and shared Studio environments. Rules stay tied to small scope, fast iteration, and checking work continuously. Treat the AI workflows as official but largely single-source. The workflow tools are new as of October 2026.

## Rules and findings
- **Start with small, simple, finishable games before a dream game** (multiple-creators): Make 10-20 small projects first (SmartyRBX). Pick simple formats (simulator, obby, hot potato, freeze tag, hide and seek, tycoon-style). Big first projects cause quitting, burnout and never ship. Zeal (Pressure) says make a small game beatable in one sitting. TheDankDeveloper says avoid FPS for a first game. Roblox staff say ship a small finished experience first (a roleplay game in under a month). Sources: [AlvinBlox 2021-01 9:17](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=557), [Roblox Creators 2023-01 16:56](https://youtu.be/vQNJztcS2AI?t=1016), [SmartyRBX 2023-10 2:08](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=128), [TheDankDeveloper 2023-10 2:03](https://youtu.be/DJsRZ2FfoMk?t=123), [SmartyRBX 2024-09 0:54](https://youtu.be/59AbBgxhfTA?t=54). Newest source 2025-11-10.
- **Ship a barebones MVP around one core loop, then iterate** (multiple-creators): Strip to core systems, map and fun; playable first, extras later as updates. Avoid feature creep and six months of perfecting before release. Tizzy: core loop plus basic progression, playtest for fun. Examples: one prison and one bank; Wacky Wizards launched with ~50-60 potions; kra_DeZ's two-baseplate game still got traction (unverified). Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 11:42](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=702), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 53:45](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3225), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 10:40](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=640), [Roblox Creators 2021-11 22:07](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1327), [SmartyRBX 2023-10 2:38](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=158). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Done is better than perfect; do not chase perfection** (multiple-creators): Perfect releases risk never shipping. Ship rough-but-playable, then polish from feedback. Messy prototype code is acceptable early. Placeholder UI is fine and gets redone. Counterpoint: Roblox staff say don't ship rushed content that testers reject (see disagreements). Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 53:45](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3225), [Roblox Creators 2021-11 41:52](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=2512), [TheDankDeveloper 2024-04 4:44](https://youtu.be/PZ3J9H-3fv8?t=284), [Daiorg 2024-10 23:42](https://youtu.be/LXRBAzLaxwQ?t=1422), [SmartyRBX 2025-06 8:07](https://youtu.be/MAnvGNgBKgQ?t=487). Newest source 2026-03-27.
- **Prototype before full development to catch flawed designs early** (multiple-creators): Discovering a flawed design 2-3 months in wastes that time. Paper prototypes (UI, controls, rules) take an afternoon; Studio prototypes for physics or risky tech take ~1-2 weeks. Keep prototypes narrow (core loop, UI/UX, key mechanics), use low-effort throwaway assets, and use them to decide whether to pivot. Prototype sound systems standalone first. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2022-02 0:13](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=13), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 2:14](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=134), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 6:48](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=408), [Roblox Creators 2022-02 9:03](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=543), [Roblox Creators 2023-08 0:19](https://youtu.be/wN4-AERPU9g?t=19). Newest source 2023-09-19.
- **Launch early, get real players and data quickly** (multiple-creators): Don't spend a year building in the dark on an unproven game. Get players in, collect data, double down on what works. Roblox deploys in seconds and streams content, so plan in days not months. Use alpha/beta or small demos (World Zero launched with 70,000 users from pre-launch hype). A competitor shipped a similar idea first while one team sat on theirs. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 24:05](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=1445), [Roblox Creators 2021-08 1:13:32](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=4412), [Roblox Creators 2022-12 11:21](https://youtu.be/0PEBFsguCdw?t=681), [Roblox 2023-09 3:22](https://youtu.be/Z-ZKqBPdl44?t=202), [Roblox 2023-09 22:44](https://youtu.be/yUDvI8mGnn8?t=1364). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Ship many small games instead of one risky long project** (multiple-creators): AlvinBlox: 10 games a year beats 1 risky unproven game. SmartyRBX: prioritise results (players and Robux) over passion; release simple games in days or a week. Roblox staff: avoid multi-year scope while learning because trends move fast. kra_DeZ says a few-day game outearned a 3-year passion project (self-reported). Sources: [AlvinBlox 2025-04 6:37](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=397), [AlvinBlox 2025-04 6:37](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=397), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 23:07](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1387), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 23:07](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1387), [SmartyRBX 2025-07 8:41](https://youtu.be/ddfqOQ7H_h8?t=521). Newest source 2026-10-11.
- **Kill or pivot failing games early** (multiple-creators): If a new game gets no players in the first week or two, consider moving on (~16 of ~20 games shelved). Rivals was reworked after launch with only a few hundred players. Scrap slow months-long projects for something small and fast. Expect most early projects to be scrapped (waveshine: 100+ scrapped files). Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-11 28:44](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1724), [Roblox Creators 2021-11 28:44](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1724), [Roblox Creators 2021-11 28:44](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1724), [Roblox 2026-04 0:44](https://youtu.be/IuJWzpvpIa8?t=44), [Roblox 2026-05 0:44](https://youtu.be/-14vhiLL4xE?t=44). Newest source 2026-07-27.
- **Don't abandon live games that still have an audience** (multiple-creators): kra_DeZ says Spin or Die lost algorithm recommendations after updates stopped. Avoid building 1-3 months of content you then abandon. Build expansions evergreen. Trend hype games, though, are quick money to leave behind. Sources: [kra_DeZ 2026-08 3:24](https://youtu.be/oMI-GhUcfFE?t=204), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 5:16](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=316), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 5:16](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=316), [kra_DeZ 2026-10 10:56](https://youtu.be/sB9W5BXjQpk?t=656), [kra_DeZ 2026-10 10:56](https://youtu.be/sB9W5BXjQpk?t=656). Newest source 2026-10-23.
- **Write a game design doc and turn it into a task board** (multiple-creators): Include genre, concept, references, features, modes, monetisation, controls, UI and needed roles/assets. Write all feature detail up front (gaps in week one beat month three). Split into Trello/Jira lists per discipline with owners and dependencies. Treat the doc as scope and as living; keep it light and flexible (SmartyRBX). Piggy Intercity spent about two months writing details. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 1:59](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=119), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 12:20](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=740), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 8:20](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=500), [SmartyRBX 2022-09 0:00](https://youtu.be/fxZ8Qv91LbY?t=0), [SmartyRBX 2022-09 2:30](https://youtu.be/fxZ8Qv91LbY?t=150). Newest source 2026-05-26.
- **Set deadlines, milestones and realistic estimates with buffer** (multiple-creators): Per-task deadlines (e.g. lobby within a week) agreed by teammates. Assume six productive hours per 8-hour day; include iteration/testing time. Solo dev takes about twice as long as planned (Zyonic). Release dates are estimates (Piggy 64 Part 2 slipped from a couple months to about a year). Brand approvals need extra lead time. Large-game example: ~7 months to alpha, ~2 months polish, ~1.5 months balancing; small teams should scope smaller. Sources: [SmartyRBX 2022-09 5:45](https://youtu.be/fxZ8Qv91LbY?t=345), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 13:23](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=803), [ItsZyonic 2026-01 3:25](https://youtu.be/4-ge-E9WvM4?t=205), [Roblox Creators 2022-05 27:41](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=1661), [Roblox Creators 2022-05 32:47](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=1967). Newest source 2026-05-09.
- **Reassess or delay unready releases; under deadline, cut scope** (multiple-creators): Push the date when testers say a feature isn't ready (one extra week, ~20% more, beats wasting prior weeks). Release when ready, not when influencers or players demand. Cancel events that aren't ready rather than burn out (Zeal shelved a 30%-complete Halloween event). Crunch is unsustainable in live ops. Under a hard deadline, skip big late features, fix critical bugs, leave a bug-fix buffer, and ship scoped-down. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 18:34](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=1114), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 17:49](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1069), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 26:04](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1564), [Creators on the Blox 2025-11 33:49](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=2029), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 26:04](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1564). Newest source 2025-11-10.
- **Graybox first, detail after playtesting** (multiple-creators): Build levels and worlds with primitives and toolbox assets to test mobility, focus and landmarks before textures/final art. Plan on paper and gray box so you don't tear down detailed work. Build map and UI first, then scripters wire systems. Build framework first, polish on top. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 26:58](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1618), [Roblox Creators 2022-03 29:28](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=1768), [Roblox 2024-09 11:32](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=692), [SmartyRBX 2022-09 4:11](https://youtu.be/fxZ8Qv91LbY?t=251), [Creators on the Blox 2026-03 1:04:56](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=3896). Newest source 2026-05-26.
- **Use a time-boxed content cadence built on existing systems** (multiple-creators): Cadence releases: no more than 2-3 weeks each; time-box and keep small to avoid burnout. No new systems, currencies or mechanics in cadence; save those for major updates/expansions. Keep tech requests small (art-heavy, tech-light); small packages of items players buy multiples of. Example: asset design to live ~3 weeks. Keep a backlog of non-holiday content for slips. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-09 9:20](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=560), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 28:11](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1691), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 0:29](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=29), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 9:15](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=555), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 27:56](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1676). Newest source 2023-08-18.
- **Plan roadmaps in sprints, with a content backlog** (multiple-creators): Plan at least three months out (2021 official advice) using 1-4 week sprints with iteration/polish time; plan two releases ahead with a longer vision. Newer Roblox guidance (2024) says quarter-to-6-month chunks, not a full year, as data changes. Track in Notion, a schedule or backlog; Roblox's own roadmap shipped 79% on schedule. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-07 6:02](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=362), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 35:04](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=2104), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 36:08](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=2168), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 18:32](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1112), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 52:24](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=3144). Newest source 2026-09-17.
- **Define asset pipelines, templates and tooling** (multiple-creators): Clear owner and handoff for design, concept, production, approval, implementation, testing. Use asset-request and string templates (owner, artist, dates, trigger). Use variables for numbers so rebalancing doesn't need re-localisation. Invest in tooling early to onboard contractors. Added systems multiply assets (attachments turned one gun into ~30 models). Wireframe UI first. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-09 30:27](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1827), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 42:09](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=2529), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 30:18](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1818), [Roblox Creators 2021-10 38:54](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=2334), [Roblox Creators 2022-01 58:15](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=3495). Newest source 2022-01-13.
- **Plan localization from the start** (multiple-creators): Put all text in localization tables early; retrofitting is painful (usernames, dialogue). Roblox auto-scrapes text so machine translation works with no string extraction; override key languages with human translators. First pass is a weekend project; getting it right takes more. Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 20:06](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=1206), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 41:17](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=2477), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 4:31](https://youtu.be/ZhtTkuaSyVs?t=271), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 5:43](https://youtu.be/ZhtTkuaSyVs?t=343), [Roblox 2023-09 28:32](https://youtu.be/yUDvI8mGnn8?t=1712). Newest source 2023-09-19.
- **Ship frequent updates and let data drive them** (multiple-creators): Tizzy: first week update daily or twice daily, form hypotheses from data; revert if the metric didn't improve. Loop: run ads, find worst stat (e.g. D1), ship fix, rerun ads. Keep updates to 2-3 goals (complex updates were least liked). After a viral launch, list urgent fixes and player requests, pick the intersection with what you want to build. Stay active 7-14 days after launch. Top experiences update about twice a week (official). Sources: [Tizzy 2026-05 16:02](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=962), [Tizzy 2026-06 0:42](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=42), [Tizzy 2026-06 15:33](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=933), [Tizzy 2026-07 4:21](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=261), [Tizzy 2026-07 18:22](https://youtu.be/LZOohZHsJus?t=1102). Newest source 2026-09-17.
- **Use safe-release engineering: flags, configs, staging, version control** (multiple-creators): Ship risky features behind runtime config flags, start at small group sizes (10% for Brookhaven's avatar editor), run as experiments with rollback thresholds, avoid overlapping experiments. Use per-developer staging and a replica test server. Use Git even solo, Wally for packages, and drafts once a team exceeds ~10 engineers. Test updates before pushing and in live-server conditions. Sources: [Roblox 2025-09 2:11](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=131), [Roblox 2025-09 13:29](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=809), [Roblox Creators 2025-12 1:04](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=64), [Roblox Creators 2025-12 13:51](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=831), [Roblox Creators 2025-12 20:18](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=1218). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Organise code structurally and keep it updatable** (multiple-creators): Leif: shared/services folders, one folder per service, server code under ServerScriptService; services own one slice each. Projects fall apart from structure, not bad code. BruceDevs: hard-to-update scripting hurt update cadence. Reuse dependency-free packages. Waveshine: build small standalone pieces (shop, inventory, save) and stack into a game; pass the change test. Sources: [Leif 2025-08 1:00](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=60), [Leif 2025-08 0:29](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=29), [Leif 2025-08 9:32](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=572), [Leif 2025-08 0:00](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=0), [Leif 2025-08 10:43](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=643). Newest source 2026-07-27.
- **Don't over-engineer; ship a working solution for one-time content** (multiple-creators): Over-building Halloween Spotlight left no time for content and got a weaker reception. Part 2 of Piggy 64 was over-engineered versus the natural Part 1. Scope events conservatively, cut to something achievable but memorable, leave slots for additions. Sources: [Creators on the Blox 2026-03 12:26](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=746), [Creators on the Blox 2026-03 40:56](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=2456), [Creators on the Blox 2026-03 1:06:20](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=3980), [Creators on the Blox 2026-05 28:48](https://youtu.be/JsiEVtWLwnA?t=1728), [Creators on the Blox 2026-03 19:19](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=1159). Newest source 2026-05-09.
- **Verify fun before adding content** (multiple-creators): Doors' first test was a white hallway with two monsters and it was fun. Build on validated mechanics (TheDankDeveloper's lava tower from an already-fun movement system). Tune feel-critical values by playtest iteration. Plan the full scope before building a sequel floor. Sources: [Creators on the Blox 2026-03 48:11](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=2891), [Creators on the Blox 2026-03 48:11](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=2891), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 3:23](https://youtu.be/5S_MYGBtmbM?t=203), [Roblox 2026-10 28:32](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1712), [Roblox 2026-10 9:10](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=550). Newest source 2026-10-23.
- **Split roles and parallelise team work** (multiple-creators): Builder does maps/lobby while coder does systems; finish models, builds and UI before bringing in the programmer (Tizzy). Don't depend on one scripter; keep backups. Hiring needs close management (ClickUp, daily calls, detailed specs). Stick to agreed scope; new ideas become new contracts. Sources: [TheDankDeveloper 2023-10 0:00](https://youtu.be/OR7xEm4iaO8?t=0), [CallMeDogey 2025-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/rHyiN2GV-J0?t=0), [Tizzy 2026-05 13:00](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=780), [Creators on the Blox 2026-05 30:11](https://youtu.be/JsiEVtWLwnA?t=1811), [Creators on the Blox 2025-11 48:08](https://youtu.be/W8ghgtipQgI?t=2888). Newest source 2026-05-26.
- **Balance via spreadsheets then playtests** (multiple-creators): Build constants/assumptions tabs referenced by formulas, export values to the game, treat as a first pass, then playtest. Balance by player time spent, not XP numbers. Budget ~1.5 months to balance a large game; weapon perks balancing took 3-4 weeks. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:19:10](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=4750), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 1:48:27](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=6507), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 39:36](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=2376), [Roblox Creators 2021-08 25:42](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1542), [Roblox Creators 2024-08 45:38](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=2738). Newest source 2024-08-07.
- **Use feedback triage and retrospectives** (multiple-creators): Run retros after every update (went well, didn't, blockers, fixes) and monitor KPIs; use anonymous tools like Slido if needed. Prioritise feedback by severity (exploits, crashes), breadth, and impact versus cost, tracked on a board (Asana, Trello, Jira). Sources: [Roblox Creators 2021-08 30:13](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1813), [Roblox Creators 2021-08 32:06](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1926), [Roblox Creators 2021-07 17:49](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1069), [Roblox 2024-09 5:25](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=325), [Roblox 2024-09 25:17](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=1517). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Monetise early and fund growth; design monetisation in** (multiple-creators): Fix retention, playtime and optimisation before revenue; build monetisation into gameplay and plan during design. Launch a basic setup and iterate on sales data (MoseyTube; his $43,292 figure is self-reported). Reinvest earnings to hire. Keep initial cost near zero until a game proves itself (BruceDevs). Sources: [Roblox 2018-07 21:58](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=1318), [Roblox 2018-07 31:14](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=1874), [MoseyTube 2026-07 5:11](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=311), [MoseyTube 2026-07 5:11](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=311), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 24:53](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1493). Newest source 2026-07-14.
- **Use AI tools step by step, not in one prompt** (multiple-creators): TheDankDeveloper: never build a whole game in one prompt; build mechanic by mechanic, playtest each step, batch good suggestions, and expect regressions in the final prompt. MoseyTube: a detailed master prompt gives a playable base but lacks depth; add your own systems and polish. Claude chat iteration works; claimed ~80% scripting time saved (self-reported). Sources: [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=40), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=40), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 5:26](https://youtu.be/5S_MYGBtmbM?t=326), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 1:21](https://youtu.be/5S_MYGBtmbM?t=81), [TheDankDeveloper 2026-01 10:56](https://youtu.be/3y7EU49_d7s?t=656). Newest source 2026-10-23.
- **Check performance, audience and pillars continuously; soft-launch** (multiple-creators): Check performance during development, not at the end. Define player goals and target audience early; don't expect 100k CCU from a niche. Check features against design pillars, which may change. Plan only the first ~20 minutes in detail (Daiorg), soft-release quietly with small ad spend, and spend ~80% effort on game, 20% on marketing. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 3:58](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=238), [Roblox 2024-09 3:58](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=238), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 43:46](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=2626), [Roblox Creators 2022-05 1:01:35](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=3695), [Roblox Creators 2021-09 43:12](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=2592). Newest source 2026-09-30.
- **Learn skills by building and focus on one** (multiple-creators): Master one skill (scripting suggested by SmartyRBX) before others; if unsure, pick at random. Build small projects, break things, search your exact error, use tutorials only for basics. Read open-source games. Expect months to years. Monetise small deliverables first. Sources: [AlvinBlox 2021-01 5:52](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=352), [AlvinBlox 2021-01 6:58](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=418), [AlvinBlox 2021-01 8:19](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=499), [waveshine 2024-10 2:54](https://youtu.be/zW0VdnOqxMk?t=174), [SmartyRBX 2025-03 1:54](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=114). Newest source 2026-07-27.
- **Use templates, existing assets and native tooling to save time** (multiple-creators): Start new games from a reusable template; marketplace/free assets save time and burnout (players didn't complain in ~10 years), though CallMeDogey's free-model game was being reworked. Studio deploys to all platforms in under 30 minutes; native Studio with drafts gives maximum velocity; Studio starts ~40% faster, Team Test ~75% quicker. When porting a live game, prove a character controller first and expect breakage; enable deferred signals and next-gen replication before server authority. Keep backups of iterations. Sources: [SmartyRBX 2025-07 5:07](https://youtu.be/vBllyv2fnWw?t=307), [Roblox Creators 2021-12 8:33](https://youtu.be/TLRakuu4vd0?t=513), [CallMeDogey 2025-03 12:54](https://youtu.be/rHyiN2GV-J0?t=774), [Roblox 2023-09 4:34](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=274), [Roblox Creators 2025-08 8:23](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=503). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Lean on built-in multiplayer and backend** (multiple-creators): Roblox handles netcode, servers and infrastructure for every experience, so skip custom backends and self-hosted servers. Put the time into gameplay. Paid servers are not required to start. Sources: [GameMakers 2020-03 11:36](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=696), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Publish once for all platforms** (multiple-creators): One publish reaches desktop, mobile and console. Expect only minor per-platform UI/UX tweaks (GameMakers notes Xbox needed none). Assets are auto-moderated, so no publishing approval queue delays the release. Sources: [GameMakers 2020-03 16:02](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=962), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Expect the first 3-4 games to flop** (single-mention): A DevForum developer expects his first 3-4 games to be near-complete flops. Stay consistent, learn why each one failed, and keep shipping. Scrapping non-working projects is a valid move, as large studios like Supercell do. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279), [Roblox DevForum 2023-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279), [Roblox DevForum 2023-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understand-the-roblox-algorithm/2772921). Newest source 2023-12-31.
- **Hold monetisation until about 10 DAU** (single-mention): Below roughly 10 DAU, don't spend effort on monetisation. Prioritise D1 retention and average playtime by iterating on design and onboarding first. Still design monetisation in, but don't optimise it yet. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2022-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688). Newest source 2022-06-08.
- **Ship a bite-size MVP in 5-6 weeks** (single-mention): A 2026 creator moved from 3-4 month builds to 5-6 week MVPs. Then live-op weekly with the community, based on player feedback. Keep the first version small enough to finish in weeks. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-04 14:40](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=880), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-04 15:21](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=921). Newest source 2026-04-06.
- **Iterate many times a day, no review wait** (single-mention): Rapid builds are cheap when there is no store review step. One 2026 creator shipped 10 builds in 3 days (Survive Bikini Bottom). Use that to test many versions and let player data pick the winner. Sources: [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-04 21:02](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=1262), [Deconstructor of Fun 2026-04 21:02](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=1262), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Guard scope against comment-driven feature creep** (single-mention): Adding obby and race modes from short-form comments expanded scope fast. The creator later said it probably wasn't a good idea. Log requests in the backlog and check them against the core loop and deadline before adding. Sources: [DitchyDevelopments 2025-11 1:27](https://youtu.be/3Pa8QmFB_TE?t=87), [DitchyDevelopments 2025-11 1:27](https://youtu.be/3Pa8QmFB_TE?t=87), [DitchyDevelopments 2026-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/O9fIqTik9ik?t=0), [DitchyDevelopments 2025-11 1:27](https://youtu.be/3Pa8QmFB_TE?t=87). Newest source 2026-03-14.
- **Prototype narrow, on paper first, then in Studio** (one-creator-repeated): Start with paper prototypes, which take minutes instead of days. Once ideas settle, prototype in Studio to catch infeasible mechanics. Scope each prototype to the key aspects being tested, not the whole feature. Scope is what separates prototyping from building. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Publish to cloud early and split large worlds into places** (one-creator-repeated): Publishing stores the data model in the cloud so you can work from any computer. Use File > Publish As with 'Add as a new place' to add gameplay areas. Split large worlds into places for streaming and teleportation. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/publish-games-and-places), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/publish-games-and-places), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/tools). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep experiences and events private until ready** (one-creator-repeated): A game saved while private stays undiscoverable until you publish publicly. Create events as private first, and switch to public only when promoting them. Private events don't show on the experience detail page. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Stage config changes and test per server** (one-creator-repeated): Test config changes in Studio before publishing to live players. Use SetTestingValue to test a value on one server only. Other servers and live players are unaffected. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Plan badge creation in batches** (one-creator-repeated): Only 5 badges per game are free per 24-hour GMT period. Each extra badge in that window costs 100 Robux. Batch badge creation, and reorder up to 50 at once by drag-and-drop. New badges appear at the end by default. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Write asset and tech requests before building** (one-creator-repeated): Scope cadence work with asset requests and tech requests before building. Separate evergreen assets from those needed each season. Keep each cadence update's effort under about 3 weeks so the schedule stays rapid. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/season-pass-design), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/content-updates). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build reusable systems for recurring content** (single-mention): Build quest, collection and event frameworks once. Future updates then cost less time and effort. This lets a small team keep up a content cadence. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Fix mature-content flags by modifying and resubmitting** (single-mention): If content is too mature for the target audience, modify the experience to fit and resubmit. Use Class.PolicyService to adjust content per audience rather than pulling the whole game. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use only owned or licensed assets** (single-mention): Use only assets you own or are licensed to use. Removed content can lead to account suspension for repeat infringement. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/ip-guidelines). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Plan paid access around its constraints** (single-mention): Paid access requires a public game with no private servers, and copying is disabled automatically. Decide the access model before building paid content. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/monetization/paid-access-robux). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Re-take the questionnaire when answers change** (single-mention): Re-take and resubmit the questionnaire whenever an update changes any answer. This is required to stay compliant with Community Standards. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/content-maturity). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Verify fixes in the next update's crash chart** (single-mention): After fixing a memory leak, check the crash chart in the next update to confirm the fix worked. Don't assume the fix landed. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/analytics/crashes). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Use native file sync instead of Rojo** (single-mention): Studio file sync gives bidirectional sync with VS Code without a separate tool like Rojo. Prefer the native option when setting up a code workflow. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 17:42](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=1062). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Add Luau tests to CI once Open Cloud testing lands** (single-mention): Write Luau test cases for packages such as a spaceship model. Run them in GitHub Actions CI/CD. Roblox says this needs Open Cloud testing, which is not confirmed as available in this source set. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 18:23](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=1103). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Learn Lua by goals and dissection** (one-creator-repeated): Don't fear Lua; it is easy to pick up with coding experience. Set incremental goals, such as a part that kills on touch, and understand why each step works. Dissect others' game code line by line, then experiment and break things in Studio. Sources: [GameMakers 2020-03 20:00](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=1200), [GameMakers 2021-01 30:02](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=1802), [GameMakers 2021-01 33:26](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=2006). Newest source 2021-01-21.
- **Try each discipline briefly before specialising** (single-mention): Run a short trial in coding, UI, 3D art and animation before choosing a specialty. Animation takes the most patience. Sources: [GameMakers 2021-01 27:14](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=1634). Newest source 2021-01-21.
- **Hold daily or twice-weekly team reviews** (single-mention): Meet daily to review completed and planned tasks. At minimum meet twice a week. This keeps parallel team work on schedule. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805). Newest source 2020-07-26.
- **Polish one core loop before adding features** (single-mention): Aim for a finished, polished simple core loop first rather than a feature-heavy game. Complexity can come after a successful title. Aim for completion over perfection. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578), [Roblox DevForum 2022-08](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578). Newest source 2022-08-14.
- **Use AI assistants for scripts and mundane tasks** (single-mention): In a Roblox demo, the assistant wrote 100% of the scripts and most environment building. Roblox also cites about +30% publishing volume, attributed to AI tools. Verify output in Studio; the source is self-reported. Sources: [Roblox 2024-09 12:45](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=765), [Roblox 2024-09 6:17](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=377), [Roblox 2024-09 21:07](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=1267). Newest source 2024-09-23.
- **Hit milestones in order: prototype, vertical slice, MVP** (one-creator-repeated): Prototype = core loop only, which aligns the team and sets the next build step. Vertical slice = one login-to-logout session. MVP = every feature needed to ship. Track which milestone you are at, and do not start art or features on an unproven loop. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2022-06 30:16](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=1816), [Roblox Creators 2022-06 35:13](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2113), [Roblox Creators 2022-06 35:13](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2113). Newest source 2022-06-11.
- **Use a cheap stand-in when a feature would take more than 2-3 weeks** (single-mention): If a feature threatens to exceed 2-3 weeks, build an intermediate or emulated version first to get an early feel for the game. Decide on the real build only after playtesting. Sources: [Roblox Creators 2022-06 55:10](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=3310), [Roblox Creators 2022-06 55:10](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=3310). Newest source 2022-06-11.
- **Get a playable within the first few days using placeholders** (one-creator-repeated): Use simple Parts and Creator Store assets as temporary stand-ins, and use placeholder cylinders or auto-hit towers where needed. Keep the prototype code reusable rather than throwaway. Playtesting in the first few days is achievable this way. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/script-game-behavior), [Roblox Creators 2022-06 28:14](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=1694). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Scope a solo beginner's first release to a 2-4 week MVP** (one-creator-repeated): Target the smallest fully working version: one map, one core loop, simple mechanics, no extras. Assume 5-15 hours per week. Polish comes later, and a finished simple game beats an unfinished complex one. Sources: [rasdev 2026-06 4:05](https://youtu.be/q0uePCbBlk0?t=245), [rasdev 2026-06 4:14](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=254), [rasdev 2026-05 5:52](https://youtu.be/N3L63uyLyQ8?t=352), [rasdev 2026-05 5:15](https://youtu.be/N3L63uyLyQ8?t=315), [rasdev 2026-06 4:05](https://youtu.be/q0uePCbBlk0?t=245). Newest source 2026-06-22.
- **Check team size and time before choosing a genre** (single-mention): Before committing to a genre, ask whether your current team and available hours can finish it. The speaker's example is that a solo developer cannot ship a full RPG in 3 months. Choose a smaller genre that fits the budget. Sources: [rasdev 2026-06 5:28](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=328). Newest source 2026-06-09.
- **Give a simulator or clone a unique hook in scope** (single-mention): A simulator built solo in about 3 weeks was feasible but judged not original enough. Scope a unique hook into the MVP from the start, rather than copying a popular concept with no differentiator. Sources: [Yann 2026-07 6:22](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=382), [Yann 2026-07 6:22](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=382). Newest source 2026-07-23.
- **Count content items before committing to art scope** (one-creator-repeated): Multiply designs by variants before starting. Example: 4 glove designs x 4 variants (including golden and charged) = 16 per world. First world gloves took days 13-25, and later sets took about a week or more each. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-03 13:38](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=818), [janisjanis01 2025-03 6:30](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=390). Newest source 2025-03-09.
- **Build only what you need now and refactor when ideas change** (one-creator-repeated): Don't architect the full game up front, since ideas change during development. Refactor freely rather than trying to guess a perfect structure. Fail fast, as the speaker put it, rather than designing a perfect system first. Sources: [B Ricey 2022-09 3:26](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=206), [B Ricey 2022-09 4:06](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=246), [B Ricey 2022-09 6:09](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=369). Newest source 2022-09-23.
- **Make it work, then make it right, then make it fast** (multiple-creators): Get a complete system working first. Don't optimise closures or performance before the game or system is complete. Bug-free code and polish are lower priority than a shipped working version at the early stage. Sources: [Crusherfire 2025-06 24:31](https://youtu.be/oUjrs6Apd7A?t=1471), [rasdev 2026-05 3:18](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=198), [rasdev 2026-05 3:18](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=198). Newest source 2026-05-03.
- **Save to Roblox every ten minutes or after any big change** (one-creator-repeated): Publishing is the only way to keep cloud-saved work, and closing Studio without publishing loses unsaved edits. If File > Publish is greyed out, stop the playtest first. Publish again after each place ID change. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/coding/test-and-save), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/save-and-publish), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/test-and-save), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building/test-and-save). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Publish before inviting testers or friends** (single-mention): Friends and playtesters only see the latest version after you publish. Publishing is also required before updates reach anyone else. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-mansion-of-wonder/invite-friends), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/going-for-a-race). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Work in a separate unpublished copy and back up before destructive edits** (one-creator-repeated): One creator accidentally deleted the main game mid-build. Duplicate the place or project for risky refactors and keep backups before structural changes. Save large modelling work often, since crashes wiped out progress. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-06 1:22](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=82), [janisjanis01 2025-03 18:35](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=1115). Newest source 2026-06-06.
- **Time-box each build step and reserve testing time** (one-creator-repeated): Set strict limits per task, such as 5 minutes for a wing or 1 minute to add a part or pick a colour. Reserve at least 5 minutes for testing each step. Change goals if a task proves too difficult. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/galactic-speedway-lesson), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-1), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/galactic-speedway-lesson), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/galactic-speedway-lesson), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-1). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Switch task when stuck, then return later** (multiple-creators): When overwhelmed or blocked, switch to a different system that sounds fun and come back later. Short relaxed warm-up tasks (e.g. a few minutes of Terrain Tools) near the end of long coding sessions keep motivation up. Sources: [Yann 2026-06 5:22](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=322), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/classroom-best-practices). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Scope each work session to one system and test it** (multiple-creators): One dev spent about 2 hours on money and save scripts. Keep each session to one system so progress is testable. Work one area at a time (gameplay, environment, assets, UI, audio) so changes are easier to evaluate. Sources: [RoDev 2025-05 4:43](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=283), [RoDev 2025-05 4:43](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=283), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build-with-assistant), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build-with-assistant). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Finish each phase before starting the next** (multiple-creators): Order work as layout, test map, code, then polish. Polishing art on an untested map risks wasted time if it must be redesigned. Playtest the layout before scripting it. Finish one sauce or milestone before starting the next. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/project-setup), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/intro-to-game-and-coding), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/greybox-a-playable-area), [Duckable 2026-08 1:15](https://youtu.be/Cc7HT3uNjeQ?t=75), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/landing). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Verify each mechanic in isolation before building on it** (multiple-creators): Print-check that events fire (e.g. Touched) before writing the harvesting logic. Test hazards with Test > Play: collect items, fall in, and confirm respawn and state reset before moving on. Break a mechanic into sub-algorithms first. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/coding-concept-algorithms), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/test-and-save). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Verify luck/RNG and server locks before launch** (multiple-creators): A launch had broken luck and a server lock that never expired. Test RNG outcomes and lock expiry explicitly before release, as these bugs are hard to catch after players arrive. Sources: [janisjanis01 2026-08 15:41](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=941), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/scripting/create-player-hazards). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Keep tunable values in GameSettings and tune them through playtests** (one-creator-repeated): Start with rough guessed values (e.g. intermission 5s, match 10s, min players 2, transition 5s) in a GameSettings module. Tune matchDuration and intermission length from playtest feedback rather than guessing. Use documented safe ranges where available. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/coding-the-game-loop), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/finishing-the-project), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/guides/configs), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/the-storm), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-galactic-speedway/customize-and-share). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build a reusable template place and re-skin it for new games** (multiple-creators): Keep a template place with your standard structure, modules, configs, lighting and services. One creator re-skinned and released a game in under a day using drag-and-drop theme swaps. Copying shop, settings and gifting systems also cut build time. Sources: [bubDEV 2025-08 2:27](https://youtu.be/Iv80MDXZBXU?t=147), [bubDEV 2025-08 2:53](https://youtu.be/Iv80MDXZBXU?t=173), [Yann 2026-06 3:39](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=219), [janisjanis01 2025-07 5:54](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=354), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Learn fundamentals without AI first, then add AI** (one-creator-repeated): Beginners should write code without AI first, then use AI once they have experience. Review AI output: it was fast for scripts but error-prone for inventory systems and needed manual fixes. Sources: [Duckable 2026-09 16:58](https://youtu.be/MBRNAqkDEFA?t=1018), [Duckable 2026-05 2:17](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=137), [Duckable 2026-05 12:15](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=735). Newest source 2026-09-12.
- **Use AI to prototype variants, then build step by step** (multiple-creators): Generate several variants of a gameplay system quickly, then keep iterating within the one that feels best. Build features one prompt at a time, testing each before the next, and ask Assistant to simplify scope when the plan is too ambitious. Sources: [Crusherfire 2026-10 3:12](https://youtu.be/4n7thZAAF-E?t=192), [Duckable 2026-09 15:08](https://youtu.be/MBRNAqkDEFA?t=908), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build-with-assistant), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/ai/build). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Complete the official beginner tutorial before custom work** (one-creator-repeated): Start with the 'Create your first game' tutorial (3D platformer where coins trade for jump power), or a Studio template such as Baseplate, before building a custom character or complex system from scratch. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/studio/next-steps), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/create-a-project), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Hand off work with a written list and review each other's code** (multiple-creators): Use a clear hand-off list between developers, since unplanned changes to an existing codebase are costly. Cross-review code to catch mistakes. Keep a shared to-do list anyone can add to and pick up from. Sources: [RoDev 2025-05 15:29](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=929), [Roblox DevForum 2023-09](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/collaboration/collaboration-best-practices), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/collaboration-best-practices). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Budget rework when plugging in a third-party framework** (single-mention): One creator had to rewrite most scripts after adding a framework to an existing game. Estimate that cost before integrating, or build the framework in from the start. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-09 0:17](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=17). Newest source 2025-09-07.
- **Ship event-style updates to refresh content faster** (single-mention): Seasonal events (e.g. an Easter event) can ship fresh content faster than building a whole new world. Use them to keep the audience engaged between major releases. Sources: [janisjanis01 2025-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/Pruto1wqxAU?t=0). Newest source 2025-03-30.
- **Plan first, then ship fast to ride a trend** (one-creator-repeated): Plan thoroughly, then release quickly to catch a trend rather than spending months developing. A one-week jam grew into a roughly three-month build with three people, so time-box the trend window. Sources: [rasdev 2026-10 7:16](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=436), [rasdev 2026-09 0:55](https://youtu.be/0_dR_081Hq8?t=55), [rasdev 2026-09 0:55](https://youtu.be/0_dR_081Hq8?t=55). Newest source 2026-10-20.
- **Judge ad experiments by day-5 benchmarks** (single-mention): From day 5, stop ads if benchmarks and the core loop are weak. Keep running them if benchmarks are good until home recommendations appear. This complements the hold-until-10-DAU monetisation rule. Sources: [Yann 2026-05 7:30](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=450). Newest source 2026-05-30.
- **Check moderation timing before planning marketplace sales** (one-creator-repeated): Uploaded items can take up to 24 hours of moderation before they can be edited and enabled for sale. Imported assets also go through moderation before joining inventory, so import well before you need them. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/publishing), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Expect a public app to stay public** (single-mention): Once an app is approved for public mode it cannot be reverted to private. Editing a public app requires resubmitting for review, so finish and test before public release. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/auth/oauth2-registration), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/chat/quick-words). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Build only what the pitch needs, then add features once the concept shows potential** (multiple-creators): Don't plan every feature up front. Build the core concept as a rough draft (plain boxes are fine), check it for about a week after finishing a feature, then add scope only if the idea works. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2017-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838), [Roblox DevForum 2017-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838), [Roblox DevForum 2017-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838), [Roblox DevForum 2021-03](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developing-expert-developers-advice-from-all-around-the-world/1134437). Newest source 2021-03-27.
- **Work on the project daily in short sessions** (one-creator-repeated): Aim for at least 30-60 minutes a day rather than seven-hour sessions twice a week. Daily contact keeps momentum and generates more ideas. Sources: [VuukStudios 2026-07 7:13](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=433), [VuukStudios 2026-07 7:13](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=433). Newest source 2026-07-29.
- **Use 15-minute focus blocks with 5-minute breaks** (single-mention): Work 15 minutes at full focus, then take a 5-minute break. Keep breaks shorter than work blocks. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/learn-to-script-learn-to-code-in-5-steps/1043259), [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/learn-to-script-learn-to-code-in-5-steps/1043259). Newest source 2021-02-12.
- **Avoid long hiatuses; never take more than 2 days off in a row** (multiple-creators): Resume on day three at the latest. Finish the full script or design before starting development, because returning after weeks makes creativity and momentum harder. Stopping work was a cause of the flops of one creator's AI-built games. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/learn-to-script-learn-to-code-in-5-steps/1043259), [Roblox DevForum 2021-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/learn-to-script-learn-to-code-in-5-steps/1043259), [Roblox DevForum 2021-12](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917), [Matt The Method 2026-08 4:41](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=281). Newest source 2026-08-26.
- **Expect slow traction and don't rush builds** (multiple-creators): One popular game took over a year to grow. A rushed project creates unforeseen problems, and a trend alone cannot keep a disorganised game alive. Invest time and effort in the build. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919), [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919), [Roblox DevForum 2017-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838), [Roblox DevForum 2017-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838), [Roblox DevForum 2016-04](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919). Newest source 2017-11-29.
- **Never extend a sprint; drop unfinished items instead** (single-mention): Keep sprints a consistent 2-3 weeks. If work is unfinished at the end, remove it from the sprint rather than delaying the sprint, and make sure each sprint shows visible progress. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475). Newest source 2020-07-26.
- **Avoid Waterfall for game development** (single-mention): Late testing misses design problems. Use short build-test-review cycles, which catch design issues early. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475). Newest source 2020-07-26.
- **Write user stories in a Who / When / Why format** (single-mention): Each story should state who the player or role is, when the action happens, and why it matters. Programmers and artists then share the same goal. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475). Newest source 2020-07-26.
- **Write numbered steps before building a feature** (multiple-creators): Break each feature into the smallest steps, e.g. get models, get spawn positions, clone model, place at position. Then decompose each step into a function, so each piece can be tested and reused. Sources: [PineappleDev 2024-11 3:25](https://youtu.be/Bgdqc4uAjtw?t=205), [PineappleDev 2024-11 0:40](https://youtu.be/Bgdqc4uAjtw?t=40), [Roblox DevForum 2023-11](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585). Newest source 2024-11-08.
- **Evaluate against fun, challenging and error-free before feedback** (one-creator-repeated): Judge the build against these three goals to decide what to fix. Keep pre-feedback improvements small, such as tweaking one obstacle rather than adding new parts. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/finishing-projects), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/finishing-projects). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Time-box playtests to about one minute with a specific goal** (one-creator-repeated): Give testers one concrete task, such as checking that a tool harvests an item as intended. Keep quick tasks to a fixed time, for example one minute to pick a colour or two minutes to playtest. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/build-an-obby-lesson). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Run a 7-30 day beta to measure D1 and D30 retention** (one-creator-repeated): A beta of at least a week is needed to collect D1 retention, and up to 30 days to see D30. Treat paid or season-pass features as beta and test their balance thoroughly before wider release. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/season-passes). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Check projected Robux earnings before committing months to a project** (single-mention): Run projected numbers through the DevEx calculator as a reality check. Do this before committing months of build time to a monetised project. Sources: [Generalist Programmer 2026-07 6:04](https://youtu.be/CFohgybeybM?t=364). Newest source 2026-07-03.
- **Stress-test a small piece at scale before building around it** (single-mention): Build one small system, push it to thousands of entities until it breaks, then iterate. Designer and programmer scale the system together. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2025-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940). Newest source 2025-10-31.
- **Optimise only when the saving is meaningful** (multiple-creators): Don't optimise prematurely. Act when the gain is substantial (one example: load time from 20 seconds to 5) or when the code runs constantly. Clean readable code is usually easier to optimise later. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258), [Roblox DevForum 2026-05](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258), [Roblox DevForum 2023-10](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-write-great-code-and-speed-up-everything/2666661). Newest source 2026-05-10.
- **Use AI for repetitive work, not for decisions or parallel code edits** (multiple-creators): AI removes repetitive work; you still choose ideas and give it requirements. Don't run several AI sessions editing the same code, since they overwrite each other. Use one main session for integration and learn enough scripting to review its output. Sources: [Matt The Method 2026-08 2:37](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=157), [MonarchDev 2026-10 5:20](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=320), [VuukStudios 2026-09 10:27](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=627), [Matt The Method 2026-08 4:41](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=281). Newest source 2026-10-26.
- **Tidy the workspace into folders at the end of each session** (multiple-creators): Use folders, groups and consistent naming, e.g. a Units folder with a child Ignore folder for attachments, and separate folders for path pieces and waypoints. Messy workspaces slow development and collaboration. Sources: [GnomeCode 2023-03 8:37](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=517), [Roblox DevForum 2023-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/movement-system/2135683), [GnomeCode 2021-12 3:27](https://youtu.be/DanjB0cTfw0?t=207). Newest source 2023-03-11.
- **Cut scope on costly features while weighing retention** (multiple-creators): When a feature is expensive, skipping it (e.g. an upgrade tree) can ship faster, but judge the retention cost first. Cut scope when a build drags past its time-box rather than letting it run on. Sources: [Raul 2026-07 4:48](https://youtu.be/CbAL1e8NgSU?t=288), [VuukStudios 2026-09 9:46](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=586). Newest source 2026-09-23.
- **Expect breaking changes while a feature is in beta** (multiple-creators): Plan for weekly API changes announced a day in advance, with change notes. Enable upcoming betas early in test places so breaking changes show up before launch. Sources: [Roblox DevForum 2026-02](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904), [Roblox DevForum 2026-01](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561), [Roblox DevForum 2020-07](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753). Newest source 2026-02-18.
- **Split the build into ordered objectives** (single-mention): Work through milestones in order, e.g. map, then pets or units, then animations, menus, monetisation, beta and ads. Build the place, then core features, then layer everything else. Sources: [MonarchDev 2026-10 1:21](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=81), [MonarchDev 2026-10 1:04](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=64). Newest source 2026-10-26.
- **Ask three peers before asking the instructor or senior** (one-creator-repeated): When stuck, redo the last few steps, then ask three peers. Only then escalate. This reduces blocking time. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-2), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/digital-citizenship/lights-and-polish), [Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer-lesson). Newest source 2026-10-28.
- **Write ideas down the same day** (single-mention): Ideas tend to be forgotten within a day or two before development starts. Log each idea immediately in a shared list or board so good concepts are not lost before planning. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-10 3:26](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=206). Newest source 2026-10-10.
- **Plan the full player loop, including edge cases, before building** (multiple-creators): Map every step of the loop, including wrong-input handling, cleanup and reset, before coding. One creator's devlog ran out of time before finishing an unplanned loop; another stresses seeing the game on paper first. Sources: [LovJak 2026-08 5:51](https://youtu.be/0RZeCn3-XKg?t=351), [RoyalDev 2026-10 0:41](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=41), [RoyalDev 2026-10 3:26](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=206). Newest source 2026-10-10.
- **Plan big updates as systems and data structures before coding** (one-creator-repeated): Design data structures for the whole game up front. Rewriting an item system that was built around items took months and forced most scripts to be rewritten. Retrofitting a new system is costly. Sources: [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 4:40](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=280), [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 3:59](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=239), [Lnor Roblox 2025-12 3:59](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=239). Newest source 2025-12-03.
- **Prototype a single system in isolation to surface blockers early** (one-creator-repeated): Test a risky system in a small standalone place, such as a multi-floor enemy navigation test, before building the full map. Once it plays well, add proper models. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2022-01 25:33](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1533), [Roox4 2023-04 2:33](https://youtu.be/Pbrvfdn9UN8?t=153), [Roox4 2023-04 5:38](https://youtu.be/Pbrvfdn9UN8?t=338). Newest source 2023-04-15.
- **Reuse older project code or parts for a barebones prototype** (multiple-creators): Pull working systems from earlier projects to shorten the first prototype. One creator built a first version in about one week this way, then iterated on feedback. Another reuses modular packages across games. Sources: [Emancyphur 2020-09 2:09](https://youtu.be/C0ybJ5ghSHk?t=129), [Last Level Studios 2020-02 12:49](https://youtu.be/HU4JJOft4Pw?t=769), [Last Level Studios 2022-01 16:57](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1017). Newest source 2022-01-07.
- **Rebuild an underperforming game instead of abandoning it** (multiple-creators): When a game's plays are declining, consider a focused overhaul with new maps and features rather than dropping it. This is a counterpoint to killing failing games early. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-08 0:02](https://youtu.be/gWKwNJRsk3s?t=2), [Mulch 2024-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/y9Ii0VEFaEU?t=0), [Last Level Studios 2020-02 12:49](https://youtu.be/HU4JJOft4Pw?t=769). Newest source 2026-08-18.
- **Replace a live game only if it is game-breaking; otherwise launch a new game** (one-creator-repeated): Keep an old game running as long as it is not game-breaking, to preserve its audience and reduce maintenance. Launch a new title rather than replacing a still-performing one (about 50k plays/week in one case). Sources: [Last Level Studios 2021-08 5:51](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=351), [Last Level Studios 2021-08 8:34](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=514), [Last Level Studios 2021-08 6:33](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=393). Newest source 2021-08-27.
- **Stay on the engine or platform that already pays bills** (single-mention): Switching engines could cost about a year to reach comparable selling speed. Only change tools when the current one blocks what you need. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2021-12 18:44](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=1124). Newest source 2021-12-09.
- **Use small tech-proof games to derive reusable pieces** (one-creator-repeated): Build small, quick games that prove individual technical pieces while drawing players, then reuse those pieces in bigger projects. Expect most accelerator-style projects not to release (about 95%); the parts still carry value. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2022-01 25:33](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1533), [Last Level Studios 2022-01 27:06](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1626), [Last Level Studios 2022-01 27:47](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1667). Newest source 2022-01-07.
- **Modularise systems into versioned packages** (single-mention): Extract lighting, permissions, commands and pets into modular, versioned packages. One creator says this lets them drop a system into a new game in about 30 seconds. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2022-01 16:57](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1017). Newest source 2022-01-07.
- **Plan tooling and GUIs early** (single-mention): A physical wall-map representation became unmanageable, so a GUI was needed sooner than planned. Decide early which content will need tooling, and build it before the content load grows. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2023-12 3:57](https://youtu.be/kQ6JMjjGvow?t=237). Newest source 2023-12-17.
- **Break problems into small parts and layer features on after each works** (single-mention): Solve one piece at a time, then add effects, damage or rewards layer by layer after each one works. This limits debugging scope and keeps each change testable. Sources: [LovJak 2025-05 0:50](https://youtu.be/M-o5hLZbjGY?t=50). Newest source 2025-05-03.
- **Outsource systems you cannot get working reliably** (single-mention): After an own attempt at a queue system failed, the creator commissioned a working version. Use a contractor when a core system keeps failing, rather than sinking more time in. Sources: [Mulch 2025-06 8:13](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=493). Newest source 2025-06-26.
- **Use Team Create with Collaborative Editing and prompt commits** (one-creator-repeated): Enable Team Create per place. Keep Collaborative Editing on so devs edit the same script via separate drafts. Commit drafts promptly; if the server changed, resolve via Merge From Server (Draft, Server or Other) before committing. Sources: [Emancyphur 2021-07 0:00](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=0), [Emancyphur 2021-07 1:46](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=106), [Emancyphur 2021-07 2:32](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=152), [Emancyphur 2021-07 4:03](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=243). Newest source 2021-07-24.
- **Preview UI components in UI Labs and organise stories** (one-creator-repeated): Install UI Labs from the marketplace to iterate on UI without launching the game. Group related components into a storybook ModuleScript with name and storyRoots set to script. Install Iris via Wally to keep it updated. Sources: [Possible Panda 2025-11 1:08](https://youtu.be/qo0x-Et5NPk?t=68), [Possible Panda 2025-11 8:11](https://youtu.be/qo0x-Et5NPk?t=491), [Possible Panda 2025-11 1:05](https://youtu.be/L-2GA_xX-SA?t=65). Newest source 2025-11-07.
- **Cut mechanics that hurt controls or tempo** (one-creator-repeated): If testing shows a mechanic makes controls clumsy or breaks high-speed tempo, cut it immediately. One creator dropped sliding for this reason. Sources: [mats-dept2 2026-10 1:13](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=73), [mats-dept2 2026-10 1:13](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=73). Newest source 2026-10-10.
- **Use AI for logic and systems, but read the code first** (one-creator-repeated): AI-assisted builds can be very fast; one creator made a game in under a week. Avoid black-box code: understand what generated code does before using it, or you cannot debug or maintain it. Sources: [RoyalDev 2026-08 7:30](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=450), [RoyalDev 2026-08 7:30](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=450), [RoyalDev 2026-08 1:22](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=82). Newest source 2026-08-29.
- **Polish after a rough playtest before wider launch** (multiple-creators): After a rough playtest, spend about two weeks fixing most bugs and adding console and mobile support. Do not send paid traffic until the core experience is polished; a rushed release hurt one creator's retention. Sources: [studio rook. 2026-08 1:56](https://youtu.be/EuaV0qc6UXQ?t=116), [Mulch 2023-04 8:17](https://youtu.be/6perjdMg3T4?t=497), [Last Level Studios 2020-02 34:41](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=2081). Newest source 2026-08-09.
- **Join game jams or hack-week sprints to prototype fast** (single-mention): Time-boxed jams force a playable prototype quickly and show whether the core idea is fun. Use them to test a concept before committing months. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2019-09 42:40](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=2560). Newest source 2019-09-13.
- **Build a monetised side project as a portfolio piece** (single-mention): A side project you can point to, ideally one that makes money, strengthens job applications more than generic credentials. Sources: [Last Level Studios 2019-09 36:33](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=2193). Newest source 2019-09-13.
- **Combine two proven genres only when design skill is still low** (single-mention): Combining two proven genres, such as obby and speed run, can give a beginner a clear hook. Choose this when learning design, not as a default. Sources: [PolarBear 2024-03 0:00](https://youtu.be/Q-Wbi1an-ww?t=0). Newest source 2024-03-06.
- **Budget post-launch updates as a permanent line item** (single-mention): Plan post-launch update time and money during full-cycle planning, not as a phase two that only happens if launch performs well. Treat ongoing updates as a standing cost of the project from day one. Source is an agency blog using self-reported GGAID data, so treat as low-advice. Sources: [StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) 2026-08](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-august-2026/). Newest source 2026-08-20.
- **Expand a proven prototype with a partner** (single-mention): A prototype can be built fast by one person (the cited original took a few days) and later expanded with a partner studio once it shows promise. Keep the prototype small and easy to show to potential partners. Self-reported indie example. Sources: [GamesBeat 2025-08](https://gamesbeat.com/?p=305182). Newest source 2025-08-00.
- **Grill yourself with AI before a significant feature** (one-creator-repeated): Before building a meaningful feature, have an AI interrogate you with pointed questions. A 10-minute, 13-question session surfaced a real design gap and produced 13 decisions. Write the answers down as decisions before any code is written. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 18:07](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1087), [Roblox 2026-10 18:48](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1128). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Review the AI's plan before it builds** (single-mention): Use plan mode so the agent writes its intent to a plan markdown file. Read and correct that plan before the agent starts building. This catches building the wrong thing before it costs time. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 2:21](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=141). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Auto-publish commits so every change is phone-testable** (one-creator-repeated): Connect commits to auto-publish through an Open Cloud key. Store the key on disk, never in chat, and set standing instructions. Roblox reports about a 1-minute prompt-to-phone-playable loop for a non-live game. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 23:16](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1396), [Roblox 2026-10 23:16](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1396). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Run Scene Analysis during the build, not just before launch** (single-mention): Check Scene Analysis continuously while building. Use it to catch issues such as unexpectedly high memory use while the cause is still small and easy to isolate. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 4:44](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=284). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Share agent skills in the project repository** (single-mention): Commit reusable agent skills to the project repo so teammates can apply them to new inputs. Example from Roblox: turning character sketches into 3D assets. This avoids each person rebuilding the same prompt workflow. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 20:31](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1231). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Build functional objects, not just meshes** (single-mention): For interactive props like doors or helicopters, add the parts that make them work, such as axles, motors and scripts. A mesh alone won't behave in-engine, so count the scripting and rig work in the estimate. Sources: [Roblox 2026-02 0:43](https://youtu.be/8Djgx0jEyfs?t=43). Newest source 2026-02-25.
- **Rebuild the foundation of a live game when it is outgrown** (one-creator-repeated): If a successful game's base can no longer support its scale, rebuild it. FIFA Super Soccer replaced its whole base about 14 months after launch, live, with millions of players already playing. This refines the rule against replacing live games, which applies to whole-game replacement. Sources: [Roblox 2026-09 38:46](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=2326), [Roblox 2026-09 38:46](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=2326). Newest source 2026-09-17.
- **Play your own build as you work** (single-mention): Use the build as your working tool and playtest as you go, not only in scheduled sessions. Roblox's advice is that this lets you spot dumb problems early, while they are still cheap to fix. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 4:44](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=284). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Don't treat build effort as a wasted tax** (single-mention): Roblox frames the effort you put into building as what makes the game yours, not a cost to minimise. Use this to resist abandoning work just because it is hard, while still applying kill or pivot rules based on data. Sources: [Roblox 2026-10 28:18](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1698). Newest source 2026-10-12.
- **Plan roadmap dates with a realistic miss rate** (single-mention): Roblox's own roadmap shipped about 79% of items on time since the last RDC. For planning, assume roughly one in five items will slip. Keep buffer in deadlines and drop unfinished items rather than extending a sprint.
- **Shorten timers in a test config to verify the loop fast** (single-mention): Temporarily cut growth, cooldown and timer values so a full loop (e.g. plant, harvest, sell) can be checked in minutes. Keep these values in GameSettings and restore them before release. Sources: [JustDucksey 2026-08 4:05](https://youtu.be/kl933X0UCC0?t=245). Newest source 2026-08-09.
