- "Sell Potatoes didn't make $43,292 because I built the perfect monetization setup on day one." ([MoseyTube 2026-07 5:11](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=311))
- "Don't make a game, make the pieces. The game finds itself later." ([waveshine 2026-07 5:47](https://youtu.be/hQ1lrFLEF1c?t=347))
- "If touching one line breaks everything and you don't know why, you didn't learn anything." ([waveshine 2026-07 6:28](https://youtu.be/hQ1lrFLEF1c?t=388))
- "As long as your game's core loop is playable, release it." ([VuukStudios 2026-07 3:07](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=187))
- "It's better to work 1 hour a day at least on your game than hopping on twice a week for 7 hours straight." ([VuukStudios 2026-07 7:13](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=433))
- "Get product in front of people and learn cheap lessons. The faster we get something in front of an audience, the quicker we can learn." ([Business of Creators 2026-08 10:30](https://youtu.be/lpIzMycpiKo?t=630))
- "The first 7 to 14 days after you publish your game are your make or break." ([MoseyTube 2026-08 5:41](https://youtu.be/xIBc5He_sy4?t=341))
- "You do not finish a game by releasing it. The actual work starts once you release the game." ([VuukStudios 2026-08 3:25](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=205))
- "I've been able to crack out slop in 1 week, hell, even 1 day, which makes spending even just 10 days working on one game feel like an eternity." ([Duckable 2026-08 16:31](https://youtu.be/3XX3Y74hzq0?t=991))
- "Watch the videos. Don't just follow them along. Don't watch them and not actually be in studio. You need to be in studio for it to work." ([RoyalDev 2026-08 2:06](https://youtu.be/7cRCq-sJa_Q?t=126))
- "There's one thing that you must do before building a Roblox game... simply just plan out your game." ([RoyalDev 2026-09 0:02](https://youtu.be/MZ-nsDEVXH4?t=2))
- "So 14 months later, they replaced it with one rebuilt from scratch live with millions of people already playing it." ([Roblox 2026-09 38:46](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=2326))
- "I literally made this game in like 3 to 4 days or something... it was getting like zero players. It was completely dead." ([kra_DeZ 2026-09 1:34](https://youtu.be/ZradA-cygP0?t=94))
- "Just seeing your game on paper before you begin helps you get it done so much better, so much cleaner." ([RoyalDev 2026-10 3:26](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=206))
- "Just seeing the date, having a fixed date on something too is like really makes you want to do it and complete it more." ([RoyalDev 2026-10 5:19](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=319))
- "Testing proved it made controls clumsy and ruined the high-speed tempo, so we cut it immediately." ([mats-dept2 2026-10 1:13](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=73))
- "Use it as your work... spot dumb things as you're going." ([Roblox 2026-10 4:44](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=284))
- "It's a lot of work, but it's totally worth it." ([Roblox 2026-10 16:57](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1017))
- "Effort that you're about to put in to building the game is not a tax. That's what makes it yours." ([Roblox 2026-10 28:18](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1698))
- "Don't spend a year working on a game that's not proven; at least get players in and get some data back." ([Roblox 2026-10 15:51](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=951))
- "never try to make a game in one prompt. You don't want to let AI do everything for you. You want to use it as a tool." ([TheDankDeveloper 2026-10 0:40](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=40))
- "90% of the success that comes from your game is the planning that happened beforehand." ([rasdev 2026-10 7:16](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=436))
- "They're just hype just for quick money grabs and then you move on to the next game." ([kra_DeZ 2026-10 10:56](https://youtu.be/sB9W5BXjQpk?t=656))
- "All games support multiplayer out of the gate. No need to write complicated backend code, no need to set up or pay for your own servers." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox))
- "Assets are automatically moderated, so you can modify and publish your game immediately across console, desktop, and mobile devices." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/why-build-on-roblox))
- "Don't wait until it's too late to pivot." ([Roblox Creator Docs 2026-10 ](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies))
- "Make sure the gameplay feels good before fleshing out a map and realizing that you wasted all your time." ([Roblox Creators 2026-10 32:01](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=1921))
- "GAME IS IN ALPHA, EXPECT BUGS AND CHANGES" ([Roblox public API 2026-11 ](https://www.roblox.com/games/93978595733734/))

## Verified additions

_Hand-added, labelled and sourced rows kept in `youtube-learn/supplements/topics/development-process-and-speed.md`; they survive rebuilds._

- **Expedited review costs 50,000 Robux per game (not 100,000)** (official, 2026-11-01): Current docs: one-time, refundable, auto-refunded 90 days after the game becomes eligible (or 90 days after payment if never eligible), forfeited only if permanently removed for Community Standards violations. 100,000 was the June 2026 launch price (creator Yann 'I would have paid the 100,000 Robux fee' is the older price). Review takes up to 48h; minor violations restart the clock. Sources: [Roblox Creator Docs kids-and-select 2026-11-01](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/kids-and-select), [DevForum staff 2026-08-19](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/highly-engaged-player-threshold-drops-to-250/4820164), [DevForum Kids/Select launch 2026-06](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-kids-and-select-global-launch-upcoming-updates-to-eligibility-ads-manager-and-expedited-review/4685717). Newest source 2026-11-01.
- **Studio agentic tooling shown at RDC26: plan mode, checkpoints, test agents** (official, 2026-09): Assistant planning mode turns text/images/sketches into a reviewable to-do list; each completed to-do is a revert checkpoint; NPC test agents are advised in batches (about 50) rather than 1,000 because of token cost; Scene Analysis recommended continuously while building (memory leaks, instance budgets). Sources: [Tech Talks EP33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8IcYpOl4Sdk), [Rapid Creation with Agentic Studio RDC26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KB5iAzy86Fo), [Scene Analysis workshop RDC26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3gnK649VNbY). Newest source 2026-09.

## Appendix: every extracted tip on this topic

- 2016-04-04 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919): Be patient: the author's popular game took over a year to become popular. Don't rush builds; invest time and effort.
- 2016-09-05 TheNexusAvenger [5:37](https://youtu.be/T5T9RMbjHBU?t=337): Limit audio length in the place (he used about 20-30 minutes) because full-length tracks produce very large data modules.
- 2017-11-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838): Don't plan every feature upfront; build only what the pitch needs and add features once the concept shows potential.
- 2017-11-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838): Build the concept as a rough draft: plain boxes are fine, and aim for a working idea over clean code since it is a prototype.
- 2017-11-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-planning-a-game-and-implementing-features/62838): Don't rush; a rushed project leads to unforeseen problems and a trend alone cannot keep a disorganised game alive.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [21:58](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=1318): Get retention, playtime and optimisation right before focusing on revenue; monetisation built into gameplay works better than bolted on.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [31:14](https://youtu.be/DWUfL8azgIk?t=1874): Plan monetisation during design and iterate; start with a store in the game, collect data and adjust prices.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [12:10](https://youtu.be/EDY-8V943IE?t=730): Ship ideas as quickly as possible so you can fail fast on metrics rather than polishing untested concepts.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [6:46](https://youtu.be/NmyzbBbrBS4?t=406): Start by joining a team if you're a weaker developer, then move to freelancing or solo work as your skills grow.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [11:29](https://youtu.be/NmyzbBbrBS4?t=689): Keep side projects running alongside a long-term game; write ideas down and hack prototypes together for a day to keep skills sharp.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [17:30](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1050): Build data-driven attack systems: add new attacks as data entries rather than writing unique code per attack.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [29:52](https://youtu.be/f8cSSzVnh9s?t=1792): Expect to write your own melee engine in Lua on top of Roblox; melee is art-intensive, requiring many custom animations.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [20:06](https://youtu.be/qoWMP-AFT08?t=1206): Put all text in localization tables from the start, even before localizing, to avoid costly refactoring later.
- 2018-07-24 Roblox [26:58](https://youtu.be/u0SxwDxFQB8?t=1618): Grey-box the level first with primitives and toolbox assets, keeping it fast and flexible, to test mobility, focus and points of interest before textures.
- 2018-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780): Always save your game offline before playtesting and publishing; Studio crashes or stops responding often, so do not trust it to keep your work.
- 2018-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780): Read 'How to actually finish a game?' and 'Planning a Game and Implementing Features' to scope and complete projects.
- 2018-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780): Study the Roblox Studio Hotkey Sheet to speed up building and scripting workflow.
- 2018-12-08 AlvinBlox [1:00](https://youtu.be/vCpl5M_9mcQ?t=60): Build a simple lobby first with transparent, non-collidable spawn locations spread around it so players spawn in varied places.
- 2019-05-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853): Test the simple blockout early and adjust layout based on play before detailing textures and props.
- 2019-05-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853): Group and name parts in Workspace as you build; it makes organising the finished map much easier.
- 2019-06-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-game/287493): Recruit collaborators from friends first, with each member specialising in a different area (building, scripting, etc.) to avoid overlap and discussion.
- 2019-09-13 Last Level Studios [42:40](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=2560): Join game jams or hack-week style sprints to build a playable prototype fast; it quickly shows whether the core idea is actually fun.
- 2019-09-13 Last Level Studios [36:33](https://youtu.be/h4W8yHxmxns?t=2193): Build a side project you can point to and, ideally, one that makes money; it strengthens job applications more than generic credentials.
- 2019-10-01 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838): Google problems first; search documentation directly for specific APIs and check the DevForum for community answers.
- 2020-02-07 Last Level Studios [12:49](https://youtu.be/HU4JJOft4Pw?t=769): Iterate on past scripts, systems and games when you see potential; rebuilding with newer techniques is an effective learning path.
- 2020-02-07 Last Level Studios [32:10](https://youtu.be/HU4JJOft4Pw?t=1930): Beginners should first build a small project such as a progress-saving obby to learn functions, events, DataStores and movement.
- 2020-02-14 Last Level Studios [34:41](https://youtu.be/_LdpUJ41cso?t=2081): Spread development out as a marathon; rushing a release reduces polish.
- 2020-03-12 TheDevKing [1:53](https://youtu.be/RmWgJVjJrqE?t=113): Publish the place to Roblox first; gamepasses can only be created for a published experience.
- 2020-03-30 GameMakers [12:18](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=738): Iterate quickly: Roblox makes publishing, launching and acquiring users fast and cheap, so test many versions.
- 2020-03-30 GameMakers [20:00](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=1200): Don't fear Lua; it is relatively easy to pick up, especially with coding experience.
- 2020-03-30 GameMakers [16:02](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=962): Publish once to all platforms; minor UI/UX changes are needed per platform, except Xbox.
- 2020-03-30 GameMakers [11:36](https://youtu.be/1q9Q6mRkciA?t=696): Roblox handles netcode and backend infrastructure, so developers can focus on gameplay.
- 2020-03-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318): Tune offsets live via the command bar, then copy the resulting values into the Offsets folder rather than hand-editing numbers repeatedly.
- 2020-04-05 AlvinBlox [6:00](https://youtu.be/DhS5oexaE3w?t=360): Store template models in ReplicatedStorage (e.g. an eggs folder) and clone them at runtime rather than leaving them in Workspace.
- 2020-05-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782): Iterate on procedural animations by editing keyframe values and easing in code rather than replaying tracks, since polishing via repeated playtests is tedious.
- 2020-05-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/perspective-object-dragging-like-superliminal/566200): Prototype quickly: write the core idea in a simple first attempt, then iterate on the maths using trial and error; the whole build took about an hour.
- 2020-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057): Standardise asset requirements (hit parts, poly count, style) in a documented guide so modelers' output plugs into code without rework.
- 2020-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057): Build a drag-and-drop framework so artists can add content themselves while programmers work on new features.
- 2020-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057): Plan with the team before building: agree on scope, standards and handoffs rather than jumping straight into content production.
- 2020-07-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/save-your-player-data-with-profileservice-datastore-module/667805): For new projects, use ProfileStore instead of ProfileService; ProfileService is stable but no longer supported.
- 2020-07-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/save-your-player-data-with-profileservice-datastore-module/667805): Use task.spawn for players already in server when the script starts, and Players.PlayerAdded/PlayerRemoving connections for the rest.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-successful-roblox-game/690805): Have the dev team meet daily to review completed and planned tasks; at minimum meet twice a week.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475): Run Agile sprints of consistent length (2-3 weeks); never delay a sprint, drop unfinished items from it instead, and ensure visible progress each sprint.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475): Avoid Waterfall for games; late testing misses design issues that Agile/Scrum review cycles would catch early.
- 2020-07-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-becoming-a-leader-for-project-management-and-team-building/690475): Write user stories in a Who / When / Why format so programmers and artists understand the goal.
- 2020-07-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753): Expect breaking changes: the author warns every current feature may be deprecated or modified in later versions.
- 2020-09-17 Emancyphur [2:09](https://youtu.be/C0ybJ5ghSHk?t=129): Build a barebones prototype fast (MiniToon made Piggy's first version in about one week) by reusing parts of older projects, then iterate on feedback.
- 2020-10-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/setup-rojo-fast-the-easy-way-to-setup-rojo-with-git-support/838182): Use an external editor (VSCode) with Rojo and Git so code is saved outside Studio and survives Studio crashes or forced updates.
- 2020-10-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/setup-rojo-fast-the-easy-way-to-setup-rojo-with-git-support/838182): Migrate an existing game to Rojo only if it is early; migrating a far-along project requires changing many code references.
- 2021-01-02 AlvinBlox [10:56](https://youtu.be/lW3BgRRUEvI?t=656): Keep shipping new games; each should improve on the last. Reviewing old projects shows how much you have learned since.
- 2021-01-17 B Ricey [1:14](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=74): Attributes were in beta in Jan 2021; enable them under File > Beta Features before use.
- 2021-01-17 B Ricey [15:47](https://youtu.be/7BSYIEdo6E4?t=947): Run one-off setup such as JSON-encoding table attributes from the command bar so values are baked in before runtime.
- 2021-01-21 GameMakers [30:02](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=1802): Learn by setting incremental goals, such as a part that kills on touch, and understanding why each step works.
- 2021-01-21 GameMakers [33:26](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=2006): Learn by dissecting other people's game code line by line, then experimenting and breaking things in Studio.
- 2021-01-21 GameMakers [27:14](https://youtu.be/y8H_GRdGaJw?t=1634): Try a short trial in each discipline (coding, UI, 3D art, animation) before specialising; animation needs patience.
- 2021-01-23 AlvinBlox [5:52](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=352): Build small projects to apply what you learn; 58% of surveyed developers did this to build knowledge.
- 2021-01-23 AlvinBlox [6:58](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=418): Break an idea into the smallest pieces, look up each piece separately, and simplify the search until you find usable code.
- 2021-01-23 AlvinBlox [8:19](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=499): Use scripting challenges such as coder advent calendars to generate small project ideas when you run out of ideas.
- 2021-01-23 AlvinBlox [9:17](https://youtu.be/fivk8yp-VT4?t=557): Start with small projects rather than huge games; big games cause loss of motivation and take months for even experienced devs.
- 2021-01-28 Emancyphur [0:18](https://youtu.be/DpBTmWHkXbo?t=18): Ship a model from the video description if your setup differs; the creator said they'd keep it updated for the current Studio version.
- 2021-02-12 Emancyphur [2:22](https://youtu.be/0B1Q2IZSlU8?t=142): Use YouTube tutorials as a supplement; practice and experiment in Studio yourself to build recall.
- 2021-02-12 Emancyphur [10:50](https://youtu.be/0B1Q2IZSlU8?t=650): Keep early projects within your skill level; a full game too early requires complex scripting and builds unnoticed bad habits.
- 2021-02-12 Emancyphur [11:31](https://youtu.be/0B1Q2IZSlU8?t=691): Repeatedly apply fundamentals in real projects until they become automatic, rather than re-studying them each time.
- 2021-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/learn-to-script-learn-to-code-in-5-steps/1043259): Use the 15-to-5 Rule: work 15 minutes with full focus, then take a 5-minute break; keep break shorter than work time.
- 2021-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/learn-to-script-learn-to-code-in-5-steps/1043259): Apply the 2 Day Rule: never take more than 2 entire days off from a project in a row; resume on the third day.
- 2021-02-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/learn-to-script-learn-to-code-in-5-steps/1043259): Keep a consolidated, searchable resource list (Google Doc or similar) and take notes on key fundamentals from each resource you study.
- 2021-03-03 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-game-have-replayability/1082534): Design for easy learning but long mastery; depth of skill keeps players playing for hours.
- 2021-03-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developing-expert-developers-advice-from-all-around-the-world/1134437): Enable Team Create so Studio saves every change; otherwise recover from File > Advanced > Open Autosaves.
- 2021-03-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developing-expert-developers-advice-from-all-around-the-world/1134437): Start with a small goal and take smaller steps; if a game is beyond your current time or skill, build something smaller first.
- 2021-06-14 GnomeCode [11:05](https://youtu.be/0rMO-VHSuGs?t=665): Set up game state in a per-character setup script on CharacterAdded: clone items and NPCs from templates to spawn points, so respawns restore everything.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [4:35](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=275): Keep one master document for all brainstorm decisions so ideas don't get lost across Discord and calls; treat design docs as living documents.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [11:42](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=702): Draw the feature's core loop on a whiteboard and cut anything not essential; ship a minimal version first to learn if it's fun before building more.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [18:34](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=1114): Push the ship date when testers say a feature isn't ready; one extra week (about 20% more time) beats shipping a rushed update that wastes the prior weeks.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [24:05](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=1445): Get the game in front of players early via alpha/beta; Red Manta's World Zero launched with 70,000 users and zero ad spend due to pre-launch hype.
- 2021-07-16 Roblox Creators [53:45](https://youtu.be/Gv5OVVyWM10?t=3225): Make new features relevant throughout the game, not just at start or end, and avoid chasing perfection; ship the MVP early.
- 2021-07-22 Roblox Creators [5:16](https://youtu.be/UL4QaSbfyC4?t=316): Don't spend 1-3 months of team resources on a feature you'll ship and abandon; build expansions to be evergreen and roll them into content cadence later.
- 2021-07-24 Emancyphur [0:00](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=0): Enable Team Create separately for each place via View tab > Team Create; close and reopen the place if it doesn't activate.
- 2021-07-24 Emancyphur [1:46](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=106): Use Team Create for real-time building; each user gets a colour shown in Explorer and selection boxes, giving instant feedback.
- 2021-07-24 Emancyphur [2:32](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=152): Keep Collaborative Editing enabled (default) so multiple devs can edit the same script via separate drafts instead of locking it.
- 2021-07-24 Emancyphur [4:03](https://youtu.be/vsHJH2bFn0Q?t=243): Commit script drafts promptly; if others committed first, resolve conflicts via Merge From Server (Draft, Server, or Other) before committing.
- 2021-07-29 Roblox Creators [1:59](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=119): Write down every feature detail before building; finding design gaps in week one beats finding them in month three.
- 2021-07-29 Roblox Creators [6:02](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=362): Plan two releases ahead and sketch what happens two years out, using a whiteboard or similar, to keep the vision focused.
- 2021-07-29 Roblox Creators [10:40](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=640): Ship a minimum viable version of the expansion first, then iterate on player feedback instead of chasing perfection.
- 2021-07-29 Roblox Creators [12:20](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=740): Break the feature into tasks (programming, art, UI, design, data) in a tool like Trello or Jira, with owners and dependencies.
- 2021-07-29 Roblox Creators [13:23](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=803): Estimate task time assuming six productive hours per 8-hour day, and include iteration and testing time in estimates.
- 2021-07-29 Roblox Creators [17:49](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1069): Reassess the release date when too many changes pile up; prioritize feedback by cost and value.
- 2021-07-29 Roblox Creators [26:04](https://youtu.be/HKLRLhHHHVc?t=1564): Release when the feature is ready, not when influencer attention spikes; the first launch impression matters most.
- 2021-08-05 Roblox Creators [25:42](https://youtu.be/pSjFYDEgRxs?t=1542): Balance in a mix of intuition and data: build from your vision, tune with average-player data, refine via playtests, then ship with analytics.
- 2021-08-12 Roblox Creators [30:13](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1813): Run team retros with what went well, what didn't, and what to improve; use anonymous input tools like Slido if people hold back.
- 2021-08-12 Roblox Creators [32:06](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=1926): Prioritise feedback by severity (exploits and crashes first), breadth of sources, and impact versus implementation cost.
- 2021-08-12 Roblox Creators [35:09](https://youtu.be/4-vZkSrGAxI?t=2109): Overhaul an existing place rather than launching a 2.0 copy when possible, since a new place strands existing players and rebuilds the base from scratch.
- 2021-08-26 Roblox Creators [1:13:32](https://youtu.be/dBlP8NdJQM0?t=4412): Ship small demos to test ideas before investing in a full game; demos reveal what sticks with less risk and allow more updates.
- 2021-08-27 Last Level Studios [5:51](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=351): Sequels vs updates: he kept the old game running as long as it wasn't game-breaking, and launched a new game rather than replacing a still-performing one (about 50k plays/week).
- 2021-08-27 Last Level Studios [8:34](https://youtu.be/3djSOjdGaE8?t=514): Replace a legacy game only if it is game-breaking; otherwise keep it running to preserve nostalgia and reduce maintenance.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [9:20](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=560): Time-box cadence development and stick to it so releases stay small and major updates still get time.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [17:32](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1052): Keep packages small and focused on items players want and will buy multiples of (e.g. cosmetics, prestige items, not one-off cars).
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [19:25](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1165): Keep tech requests small: prefer tech-light, art-heavy items like furniture and avatar cosmetics; avoid scripted encounters and raids.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [28:11](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1691): Don't spend more than two to three weeks on a single cadence release; larger impactful releases deserve the time.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [30:27](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=1827): Define an asset pipeline with clear owners and handoffs at each stage: design, concept, production, approval, implementation, testing.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [35:04](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=2104): Plan roadmaps at least three months out, using one-to-four-week sprints with built-in iteration and polish time.
- 2021-09-02 Roblox Creators [42:09](https://youtu.be/kQIyqMdh7Os?t=2529): Use string and asset request templates; use variables for numbers so rebalancing doesn't need re-localisation.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [20:17](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=1217): Plan UI in a fixed workflow: wireframe, then style sheet, then icons and element styling, before implementing.
- 2021-09-09 Roblox Creators [41:17](https://youtu.be/AG-3o_GdqKI?t=2477): Plan localisation from the start; retrofitting it is painful, especially for custom usernames and dialogue text formatting.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [3:14](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=194): Season passes let you frontload content so the team has time to build the next pass instead of a weekly content rat race.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [8:20](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=500): Write a feature design doc with overview, goals, UI preview, systems rules, examples, asset requests and future updates before building.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [39:36](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=2376): Keep XP values as flavor; balance by player time spent per mission, since the number of missions drives effort, not the XP number.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [1:19:10](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=4750): Balance in a spreadsheet first, with constants and assumptions tabs referenced by formulas, then export values to the game and keep them in sync.
- 2021-09-16 Roblox Creators [1:48:27](https://youtu.be/k2gbQI-ggB4?t=6507): Treat the balance sheet as a first pass; playtest, collect player feedback, then iterate the tuning.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [43:12](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=2592): Define a few design pillars (positive message, social, low friction) and check each new feature against them.
- 2021-09-30 Roblox Creators [45:53](https://youtu.be/UlKAoiFLgUo?t=2753): Pillars can change mid-development if players move in a different direction; don't treat them as fixed.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [0:29](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=29): Build cadence content only on existing systems; introduce new features, currencies or mechanics in major updates first.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [9:15](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=555): Time-box cadence development and keep releases small so the team can repeat them without burning out.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [27:56](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1676): Do not spend more than two or three weeks on one cadence release; that time is better spent on larger releases.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [30:18](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=1818): Define an asset pipeline with a clear owner and handoff for each stage: design, concept, production, approval, implementation, testing.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [36:08](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=2168): Plan releases at least three months out; use 1-4 week sprints with built-in iteration and testing time.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [38:54](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=2334): Use asset request and quest string templates to record owner, artist, dates, description and trigger; this reduces miscommunication.
- 2021-10-21 Roblox Creators [55:13](https://youtu.be/d4g30UupgKw?t=3313): Keep a backlog of non-holiday cadence content to release when production slips.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [18:16](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=1096): Build the task list methodically: enumerate every plantable, harvestable, feedable and craftable item so each content piece has a quest task.
- 2021-11-11 Roblox Creators [19:38](https://youtu.be/iWSZJSE2NEc?t=1178): Check that harvested items feed your crafting system before writing quests, so you don't paint yourself into a corner without ingredients.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [22:07](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1327): Launch with a small scope (Wacky Wizards shipped with about 50-60 potions) and expand weekly based on what players actually do.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [28:44](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=1724): If a new game gets no players in its first week or two, consider ditching it; the speaker shelved about 16 of roughly 20 games that way.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [34:09](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=2049): Write every idea into a backlog and avoid swapping projects constantly; the speaker used to change projects every two weeks and lost progress.
- 2021-11-18 Roblox Creators [41:52](https://youtu.be/rVr0IyfYkPg?t=2512): Prototype fast and accept messy code; get it out so players can play it, since code quality matters less than getting feedback.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [8:33](https://youtu.be/TLRakuu4vd0?t=513): Use purchased or free marketplace assets to save time and avoid burnout; players did not complain about it in ~10 years.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [4:31](https://youtu.be/ZhtTkuaSyVs?t=271): Industry localization is slow (weeks to find translators); Roblox auto-scrapes game text to its cloud, so you can enable machine translation without manual string extraction.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [5:43](https://youtu.be/ZhtTkuaSyVs?t=343): Use machine translation as default and override specific languages with human translators via developer portal collaborators (individuals or groups) where quality matters.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [19:57](https://youtu.be/bKvOPfp3oX0?t=1197): Predict trigger timing by monitoring player counts; they ramp up with excitement, so watch closely near the expected moment.
- 2021-12-01 Roblox Creators [3:31](https://youtu.be/cY1B5_x4RoU?t=211): Wireframe UI before building it; it communicates to teammates, exposes issues early, and saves hours of rework.
- 2021-12-03 Roblox Creators [43:46](https://youtu.be/hD2Ive5RqtE?t=2626): Define player goals early in design to keep scope focused. It is still useful to revisit them on a live game to focus future updates.
- 2021-12-09 Last Level Studios [2:05](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=125): Tankery's CSG-injected OBJ tank models kept each tank to ~10 parts, enabling 20v20 battles with low lag.
- 2021-12-09 Last Level Studios [18:44](https://youtu.be/v_TGu45AD_g?t=1124): Stay on the platform you already know when it pays bills; switching engines could cost about a year to reach comparable selling speed.
- 2021-12-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917): Finish writing the full script before starting development; avoid putting the script on hiatus, as returning after weeks makes creativity harder.
- 2021-12-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917): Never build overly complex projects at once; start with small steps and progress at your own pace.
- 2021-12-14 GnomeCode [0:14](https://youtu.be/DanjB0cTfw0?t=14): Use a fixed path with manually placed waypoints for tower defense; it avoids building many invisible walls for PathfindingService.
- 2021-12-14 GnomeCode [3:27](https://youtu.be/DanjB0cTfw0?t=207): Organise path pieces and waypoints into separate folders in Workspace to keep the map manageable.
- 2021-12-17 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/you-need-to-use-the-knit-game-frameworka-template-to-help-get-started-using-knit/1592333): Use Rojo to bring professional software engineering tooling into Roblox development.
- 2022-01-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginners-guide-on-scripting-animefighting-vfxvisual-effects-part-1/1610853): Keep client-replicated VFX assets in ReplicatedStorage and clone then delete them after use to avoid leaking instances.
- 2022-01-07 Last Level Studios [16:57](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1017): Extract systems (lighting, permissions, commands, pets) into modular, versioned packages so you can drop them into new games in about 30 seconds.
- 2022-01-07 Last Level Studios [25:33](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1533): Build small games that prove out individual tech pieces while drawing players, then reuse the pieces in bigger projects.
- 2022-01-07 Last Level Studios [27:06](https://youtu.be/v8S5puPQEw4?t=1626): Expect most accelerator-style projects not to release; the learning still has value, and pieces can be reused in future games.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [2:20](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=140): Build a content backlog before launch so new content can ship at any time and absorb contractor delays; Bad Business lacked one early and regretted it.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [10:16](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=616): Expect added systems to multiply asset work: the attachment system turned one gun model into ~30 models, slowing each gun's implementation.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [18:32](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=1112): Track the content log in Notion: chronological checklist, tagged by cadence vs major, content type and team member; plan months ahead and update constantly.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [52:24](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=3144): Write planned items into an internal schedule so ideas are not forgotten days later.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox Creators [58:15](https://youtu.be/oV8DKuAjQQs?t=3495): Invest in tooling early for team expansion; good tools make onboarding new contractors far easier than teaching custom workflows.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-2-characters-and-play-it-in-game-like-a-takedown-animation/1625809): Name each rig in Moon Animator and its exported animation (e.g. main/enemy) so you can tell which animation belongs to which character.
- 2022-01-13 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-2-characters-and-play-it-in-game-like-a-takedown-animation/1625809): Wait for the main character's animation to finish rather than the enemy's before restoring control.
- 2022-02-03 Roblox Creators [0:13](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=13): Prototype before full development; discovering a flawed design 2-3 months in wastes that whole period.
- 2022-02-03 Roblox Creators [2:14](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=134): Use paper prototypes for UI, controls, and game rules; they can be built in an afternoon and iterated by erasing and redrawing.
- 2022-02-03 Roblox Creators [6:48](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=408): Use studio prototypes to test physics-heavy or technically risky mechanics early so you can pivot sooner if they fail.
- 2022-02-03 Roblox Creators [9:03](https://youtu.be/iI2t7Yd7Byc?t=543): Keep prototypes narrow: test the core loop, UI/UX, key mechanics, and game rules, not the whole feature.
- 2022-02-17 Roblox Creators [56:35](https://youtu.be/BkQL2_DY9Mc?t=3395): Keep updates simple with two or three goals; their most complex updates were often least well received.
- 2022-03-01 GameMakers [16:23](https://youtu.be/t-jpyaQffYE?t=983): Start ideation from the game itself: break it into selling points and what the player can expect to do and have the game do for them.
- 2022-03-17 Roblox Creators [29:28](https://youtu.be/czVxazdl0kg?t=1768): Plan on paper and gray box before detailing; detailing first forces costly teardown when a passage or tube must move.
- 2022-04-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/benis-pathfinding-customizable-easy-to-use-auto-animating-r6-r15-support/1759994): Insert the script into an unanchored character; anchored characters will not move with the pathfinding.
- 2022-04-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/benis-pathfinding-customizable-easy-to-use-auto-animating-r6-r15-support/1759994): If an updated model still runs the old version, delete it from inventory, re-purchase it, and re-insert the script.
- 2022-05-05 Roblox Creators [1:01:35](https://youtu.be/xBlHz30PMKo?t=3695): Set a realistic target audience early and scope the game to it; don't expect 100k CCU from a niche audience.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox Creators [27:41](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=1661): Brand partnerships take longer than internal projects; plan extra lead time for brand reviews and approvals, and be cautious about polish standards.
- 2022-05-12 Roblox Creators [32:47](https://youtu.be/14RMDByrG1U?t=1967): Add buffer to schedules for revisions and approval windows; one team works in two-week sprints and found under 3-5 days or fewer than 2 revisions caused trouble.
- 2022-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-proper-open-source-project-with-devops/1796689): Develop on a dev branch and only merge to main when production-ready; pushes to main trigger publishing and release creation.
- 2022-05-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-proper-open-source-project-with-devops/1796689): Use Rojo's live sync (rojo serve) with the Rojo Studio plugin to test code changes in Studio while editing in your text editor.
- 2022-06-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-monetization-metrics-for-the-analytics-dashboard/1826688): Don't focus on monetization below ~10 DAUs; prioritise D1 retention and average playtime by iterating on design and onboarding first.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [19:42](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=1182): Prototype early (first 2-3 weeks) to find flaws, fun and technical roadblocks before building maps, art and features on top.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [22:43](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=1363): Use paper prototypes for fast design iteration and UI/mobile layout testing; use studio prototypes for physics, AI and technical risk.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [28:14](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=1694): Build studio prototypes with reusable code, not throwaway hacks, though temporary placeholders (cylinders, auto-hit towers) are fine.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [30:16](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=1816): Prototype the core loop first. It aligns the team and tells artists and programmers what to build next.
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [35:13](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=2113): Milestones: prototype (core loop), then vertical slice (one login-to-logout session), then MVP (all features needed to ship).
- 2022-06-11 Roblox Creators [55:10](https://youtu.be/Mep4_w8qxeE?t=3310): If a feature would take more than 2-3 weeks, use a cheap intermediate solution to get an early feel for the game.
- 2022-08-09 Suphi Kaner [19:12](https://youtu.be/c4DymnlVuL0?t=1152): Duplicate the place's scripts and only change the target place IDs, rather than rebuilding each place from scratch.
- 2022-08-14 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-art-of-robux-getting/1922578): Aim for a finished, polished simple core loop first rather than a feature-heavy game; complexity can come after you have a successful title.
- 2022-09-10 SmartyRBX [0:00](https://youtu.be/fxZ8Qv91LbY?t=0): Write a game design document (Google Docs is fine) covering genre, concept, reference games, features, modes, monetisation, controls, UI, and required assets/roles.
- 2022-09-10 SmartyRBX [2:30](https://youtu.be/fxZ8Qv91LbY?t=150): Turn the design document into a task board (Trello or checkbox list) with one list per discipline such as UI, scripting, building, modeling, animation.
- 2022-09-10 SmartyRBX [4:11](https://youtu.be/fxZ8Qv91LbY?t=251): Build the map and UI first, then have scripters wire up systems, keeping Studio organised so updates stay manageable.
- 2022-09-10 SmartyRBX [5:45](https://youtu.be/fxZ8Qv91LbY?t=345): Set deadlines on individual tasks (e.g. build the lobby within a week) rather than only a final release date, and get teammates to agree to them.
- 2022-09-20 Roblox [2:07](https://youtu.be/jULYHFTmq4s?t=127): Team up with a coder if you can model and design; Naomi found a coder and co-built Infection, Inc., which became second largest on the platform for a couple months.
- 2022-09-23 B Ricey [3:26](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=206): Only build what you need now (YAGNI); don't architect the full game upfront since ideas change during development.
- 2022-09-23 B Ricey [4:06](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=246): Refactor freely; it costs less time than guessing a perfect structure up front. Fail fast like SpaceX rather than designing like NASA SLS.
- 2022-09-23 B Ricey [17:05](https://youtu.be/MyK4o2pIpQw?t=1025): Release a game that mostly works and players enjoy; a perfect codebase that never ships earns nothing.
- 2022-10-14 Emancyphur [2:31](https://youtu.be/SdCmo91rsVc?t=151): Strengthen basics in scripting, building, and animation; continuously learn from each problem and mistake encountered.
- 2022-12-01 Roblox Creators [11:21](https://youtu.be/0PEBFsguCdw?t=681): Roblox lets you deploy and iterate in days: servers, networking and infrastructure are handled, so you can test ideas fast and scrap failing ones.
- 2023-01-12 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/movement-system/2135683): Organize units in a Workspace folder (Units) and use a child Ignore folder for attachments, keeping workspace structure consistent.
- 2023-01-26 Roblox Creators [16:56](https://youtu.be/vQNJztcS2AI?t=1016): Ship a small, finished experience first (a simple roleplay game took under a month) rather than starting with your dream game; it attracts collaborators.
- 2023-01-28 SimTek Game Development [12:26](https://youtu.be/tjwI4b2ZRZk?t=746): Build a simple test arena with a blocked path and a trigger plate to verify recomputation before adding real gameplay.
- 2023-02-08 SmartyRBX [1:19](https://youtu.be/rdWyuDEpGyk?t=79): Develop efficiently and communicate directly with your team so you can re-release updates as fast as possible.
- 2023-02-23 Roblox Creators [22:01](https://youtu.be/BsyU-SJ9L-c?t=1321): Study how other Roblox and mobile games solve hard problems like vehicle controls; expect multiple redesigns (Cindering redesigned vehicle controls 6-7 times).
- 2023-03-11 GnomeCode [8:37](https://youtu.be/QRwTkEPPChM?t=517): Tidy the workspace at the end of each build session using folders, groups and consistent naming; messy workspaces greatly slow development and collaboration.
- 2023-04-12 Mulch [8:17](https://youtu.be/6perjdMg3T4?t=497): A rushed release hurt retention; polish the core experience before sending paid traffic.
- 2023-04-14 TheDankDeveloper [0:00](https://youtu.be/HZLsTYgFo4c?t=0): Start with simple primitives (8-sided cylinder) and extrude/scale to build low-poly terrain quickly; keep it simple for beginners.
- 2023-04-15 Roox4 [2:33](https://youtu.be/Pbrvfdn9UN8?t=153): Prototype risky mechanics first (e.g. enemy navigating a multi-floor building) before building the full map, to find blockers early.
- 2023-04-15 Roox4 [5:38](https://youtu.be/Pbrvfdn9UN8?t=338): Get new enemy behaviour working in-engine with placeholder cylinders before investing in modelling, then model once it plays well.
- 2023-05-11 Roblox Creators [2:07](https://youtu.be/3Y4_B5-EHjY?t=127): Keep building quality games across different genres to strengthen your portfolio and platform knowledge.
- 2023-05-18 GnomeCode [31:14](https://youtu.be/TJnV2ycnXZc?t=1874): Finish projects rather than leaving many 'coming soon' games unfinished; the creator names this as his main ongoing weakness.
- 2023-06-29 ItsZyonic [0:00](https://youtu.be/ODh2jPEGWUc?t=0): Get the initial player kick from social media; TikTok is the speaker's preferred channel, with YouTube Shorts and other followings as secondary outlets.
- 2023-07-30 GnomeCode [1:22](https://youtu.be/UsPYgIW3rv8?t=82): Avoid duplicating scripts into every model; keep one script and update all copies from it, so changes are made once.
- 2023-08-12 Roblox Creators [0:19](https://youtu.be/wN4-AERPU9g?t=19): Prototype feature ideas before full build to catch design flaws, oversights and technical limits early instead of months into development.
- 2023-08-12 Roblox Creators [0:19](https://youtu.be/wN4-AERPU9g?t=19): Use prototyping to decide whether to pivot if your vision can't be built within your means.
- 2023-08-12 Roblox Creators [0:50](https://youtu.be/wN4-AERPU9g?t=50): Keep prototypes fast and narrow: test only the aspects you care about, not the full feature.
- 2023-08-12 Roblox Creators [0:50](https://youtu.be/wN4-AERPU9g?t=50): Make prototype assets low effort and easy to produce so throwaway work stays cheap.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox Creators [4:34](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=274): Build content cadence on existing systems instead of new ones. New systems add bugs and create an unsustainable treadmill.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox Creators [11:51](https://youtu.be/kNE757v3Bwg?t=711): Use major expansions for team morale too. They give the whole team a creative challenge beyond routine content.
- 2023-08-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-successfully-grow-your-game-ft-senseideveloper-vikinglaw-promoting-and-advertising-your-game/2535279): Expect the first 3-4 games to be near-complete flops; stay consistent, learn why they fail, and keep building.
- 2023-08-25 1Coal [0:20](https://youtu.be/YBSsksqfxo0?t=20): Start with a small game like an Obby or tycoon you can finish in a few weeks; ambitious clones of big games take years and large budgets.
- 2023-09-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333): Split work with teammates and review each other's code to catch mistakes.
- 2023-09-12 janisjanis01 [3:34](https://youtu.be/9aQpeTCZ7yM?t=214): When a custom system stalls for hours, follow a known tutorial for a working baseline, then extend it.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [18:19](https://youtu.be/JjkAaXVULEk?t=1099): Plan creator collaborations far ahead for big events; one championship tie-in was planned 12-14 months in advance.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [16:59](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1019): Plan launches and platform timing in advance; brand calendars are finite, and development slips push campaigns into conflict.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [28:06](https://youtu.be/S8ORVEKec_4?t=1686): Ship rapidly with simple builds; Roblox cycles are closer to TikTok than mobile or AAA, so set expectations with partners accordingly.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [19:20](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=1160): Prototype sound systems in a standalone concept first, prove them, then integrate into the game; account for Roblox sound-trigger delay.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [4:34](https://youtu.be/UVq0045HNQ4?t=274): Use off-platform tools (Blender, Substance Painter, Adobe) for asset creation, then import into Roblox.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [5:28](https://youtu.be/V1uFFUUCLpo?t=328): Analyze other games for what makes them succeed or fail before building your own, per the Animal Life Forest Roleplay creator.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [3:22](https://youtu.be/Z-ZKqBPdl44?t=202): Roblox updates are instant and feedback fast, so plan development in days rather than months as on mobile.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [22:44](https://youtu.be/yUDvI8mGnn8?t=1364): Iterate fast: Roblox streams content and handles backend, so you can test hypotheses and get feedback almost instantly, then double down on what works.
- 2023-09-19 Roblox [28:32](https://youtu.be/yUDvI8mGnn8?t=1712): Design for global audiences: check device types, support languages, and use machine translation to cover most of the way to other languages.
- 2023-10-14 TheDankDeveloper [0:00](https://youtu.be/mwu3k4nXYZQ?t=0): Set a hard time limit and cut scope early; the creators hit scripting errors and had only about 10 minutes left before testing.
- 2023-10-14 TheDankDeveloper [2:56](https://youtu.be/mwu3k4nXYZQ?t=176): Leave a final buffer for bug fixing; last-minute errors consumed the final 10 minutes.
- 2023-10-15 SmartyRBX [2:08](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=128): Make simple games first, earn Robux and community experience from them, then build more advanced games.
- 2023-10-15 SmartyRBX [2:38](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=158): Build a minimum viable product: a playable, fun version with only bare-bones features (e.g. one prison and one bank, not many locations).
- 2023-10-15 SmartyRBX [4:24](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=264): Develop gradually: ship small, then iterate on the game instead of building the entire game at once.
- 2023-10-15 SmartyRBX [4:46](https://youtu.be/315hkxjTBZc?t=286): Use Roblox's fast update cycle (upload in seconds, update in seconds) to test ideas with real players and iterate repeatedly.
- 2023-10-20 TheDankDeveloper [2:03](https://youtu.be/DJsRZ2FfoMk?t=123): Make a game you're capable of finishing; for a first game avoid complex genres like FPS and try something simpler like a simulator.
- 2023-10-20 TheDankDeveloper [2:03](https://youtu.be/DJsRZ2FfoMk?t=123): Plan the game concept, what you need to build, and how you will monetise it before starting.
- 2023-10-23 SmartyRBX [13:17](https://youtu.be/vemqKJ-5yRE?t=797): Build a minimum viable product: strip to core systems, maps and fun, and make it playable before adding features.
- 2023-10-23 SmartyRBX [15:36](https://youtu.be/vemqKJ-5yRE?t=936): Pivot after testing: refine the edges and add what players want, staying within your scope, budget and Roblox limits.
- 2023-10-23 SmartyRBX [17:39](https://youtu.be/vemqKJ-5yRE?t=1059): Keep updating the game as a living product; improving monetisation, gameplay and maps over time drove Sussy Imposter Land's growth.
- 2023-10-23 SmartyRBX [21:02](https://youtu.be/vemqKJ-5yRE?t=1262): Keep the game design document light and flexible, since rigid GDDs block the iteration Roblox updates allow.
- 2023-10-25 TheDankDeveloper [0:00](https://youtu.be/OR7xEm4iaO8?t=0): Split work between collaborators: one builds map assets and enemies, the other builds placement and GUI systems, to parallelise a short build.
- 2023-10-29 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-write-great-code-and-speed-up-everything/2666661): Clean code is usually faster and easier to optimise where it matters; avoid over-optimising at the cost of readability.
- 2023-11-02 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585): Decompose problems into small tasks, then subtasks; each subtask becomes its own function to enable reuse and easier future changes.
- 2023-11-28 ItsZyonic [0:00](https://youtu.be/IPqBHaZNmNA?t=0): Studio Light lets you build simple Roblox games on a phone, but it lacks multiplayer, so plan single-player scope.
- 2023-11-28 ItsZyonic [2:57](https://youtu.be/IPqBHaZNmNA?t=177): Typing code on a phone is very slow; use a computer with a real keyboard for scripting work.
- 2023-12-17 ByteBlox [0:41](https://youtu.be/9YwYDuIuih4?t=41): Learn debugging early: the speaker argues being able to fix errors is what keeps beginners motivated to keep learning.
- 2023-12-17 Last Level Studios [2:36](https://youtu.be/kQ6JMjjGvow?t=156): Iterate on feedback from the community quickly; SCR's signal prototype grew into a full rebuild within days of a simple fix.
- 2023-12-17 Last Level Studios [3:57](https://youtu.be/kQ6JMjjGvow?t=237): Plan for tooling early: a physical wall-map representation became unmanageable, so a GUI was needed sooner than planned.
- 2023-12-25 VuukStudios [16:24](https://youtu.be/fEcFzlBQew8?t=984): Only build your own game after you have an established reputation and a trusted team; he says it took him years.
- 2023-12-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understand-the-roblox-algorithm/2772921): Be willing to scrap projects that do not work, as large studios like Supercell do.
- 2024-01-06 B Ricey [1:00](https://youtu.be/NDgnMeJ4CbM?t=60): Store shared templates in ReplicatedStorage so both client and server can clone or read them.
- 2024-01-20 B Ricey [0:40](https://youtu.be/92acKqS_Jo0?t=40): Build the placement system in two passes: first client-side snapping, then server validation, since the server must verify all placement positions.
- 2024-02-17 B Ricey [2:48](https://youtu.be/FaEMgVDlaNQ?t=168): Keep the server script thin and push logic into single-purpose ModuleScripts (PlotManager, PlotSpawnPool, ClientPlacer) for readability and expandability.
- 2024-02-17 B Ricey [21:52](https://youtu.be/FaEMgVDlaNQ?t=1312): Create and destroy a fresh client object on each toggle for a clean state instead of managing persistent flags.
- 2024-03-02 B Ricey [22:17](https://youtu.be/SVBmZ3yIMRo?t=1337): Always clean up in the destroy path (unbind render step and input actions) or the build tool leaks behaviour after toggling off.
- 2024-03-06 PolarBear [0:00](https://youtu.be/Q-Wbi1an-ww?t=0): Combine two proven genres (Obby and speed run) into one game when the creator is still learning design.
- 2024-03-09 SimTek Game Development [0:00](https://youtu.be/H7sk_T1zrD8?t=0): Check TextChatService's ChatVersion property in Explorer; new projects default to TextChatService, switch to Legacy only for old code.
- 2024-04-16 TheDankDeveloper [4:44](https://youtu.be/PZ3J9H-3fv8?t=284): Ship a placeholder first (roll button, layout) and polish later; the creator says the UI is placeholder and redone multiple times.
- 2024-06-09 AlvinBlox [2:12](https://youtu.be/PC14bFNer74?t=132): Store the template GUI in ServerStorage and clone it per player instead of keeping it in the workspace.
- 2024-06-16 janisjanis01 [5:47](https://youtu.be/NWyK_PbFdxE?t=347): Start with a small project and learn from mistakes. Avoid months-long projects for a first game.
- 2024-06-30 janisjanis01 [10:18](https://youtu.be/JeyYzEjPjc8?t=618): Speedrun areas with a one-day deadline by building in batches; build montages can be used for social content.
- 2024-06-30 Mulch [0:00](https://youtu.be/pqCXX06vlDw?t=0): Ship a simple, novel idea fast: a multiplayer version of a popular genre (3-player Obby) was built in about 5 minutes using free models.
- 2024-07-19 Mulch [0:40](https://youtu.be/y9Ii0VEFaEU?t=40): Reuse start/finish area templates from earlier games to speed up building a new obby map.
- 2024-08-07 Roblox Creators [10:49](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=649): Set milestones: about 7 months to an alpha with core gameplay, then about 2 months of polish, bug fixing and balancing before launch.
- 2024-08-07 Roblox Creators [45:38](https://youtu.be/2t_zhxyLOJ8?t=2738): Budget about a month and a half for balancing a large-scope game; with small teams and budgets, design a smaller scope.
- 2024-09-19 GnomeCode [0:35](https://youtu.be/oEWoyT-GBZY?t=35): Enable Output window before coding so errors are visible while building systems step by step.
- 2024-09-20 GnomeCode [0:00](https://youtu.be/SUZPuQrKJuU?t=0): Name UI image buttons exactly as the map models in ReplicatedStorage.Maps; a spelling mismatch silently breaks the vote lookup.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [5:25](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=325): Use a project management board (Asana, Monday, Trello, Jira) to prioritise and track player feedback so live-ops doesn't get chaotic.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [19:50](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=1190): Plan the roadmap in quarter-to-6-month chunks, not a full year, and embrace iteration as player data changes.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [23:14](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=1394): Use per-developer staging environments, merge features into a release branch, and deploy to a replica test server before launch.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [25:17](https://youtu.be/DxrLfwsfV1I?t=1517): Hold a retrospective after every update covering what went well, what went wrong, blockers and fixes, then monitor the KPI goals set for the feature.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [4:06](https://youtu.be/ETDit6eO4sQ?t=246): Run the flywheel: build, promote (word of mouth, social, influencers, sponsored and search ads), earn, then iterate on engagement and monetization.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [32:26](https://youtu.be/NEK7jq1LFu8?t=1946): Beginners: start with free 3D modeling software and basic tutorials, then find other UGC creators for plugins and tips.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [17:42](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=1062): Use Studio file sync to edit scripts in VS Code with bidirectional sync, instead of a separate tool like Rojo.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [18:23](https://youtu.be/XrxUy51NsvI?t=1103): Once Open Cloud testing lands, write Luau test cases and run them in GitHub Actions CI/CD for automated testing of packages like a spaceship model.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [3:58](https://youtu.be/cWDNVvOh4o0?t=238): Check performance continuously during development rather than doing an optimization pass at the end.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [11:32](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=692): Graybox your world first and playtest it so you can move landmarks and props before investing in final art.
- 2024-09-23 Roblox [34:46](https://youtu.be/vWx8UTxsi0A?t=2086): Use a grid-based board like Milanote to lay out references and tasks; switch to Jira when the project gets more demanding.
- 2024-09-29 SmartyRBX [0:54](https://youtu.be/59AbBgxhfTA?t=54): Make simple small games first; don't start massive projects. Build 10-20 small projects to match skill level before tackling dream games, since big projects lead to quitting.
- 2024-10-05 TheDankDeveloper [4:25](https://youtu.be/F2vhC3AuZyk?t=265): Under a tight deadline, skip big new features; fix critical bugs and ship. Late additions risk breaking the build.
- 2024-10-12 waveshine [2:54](https://youtu.be/zW0VdnOqxMk?t=174): Learn scripting by reading open-source Roblox games and working out what each script and function does; check the code is well written first.
- 2024-10-20 Daiorg [23:42](https://youtu.be/LXRBAzLaxwQ?t=1422): Ship rough-but-playable updates and keep refining; the speaker expects polish to come as the developers finish the game.
- 2024-10-27 janisjanis01 [2:09](https://youtu.be/ZyQ73kfh_b4?t=129): Remaking a stalled game from scratch with a fresh design can beat continuing to update a dead one; he rebuilt his first game in 5 days.
- 2024-11-01 Roox4 [3:48](https://youtu.be/LC8AV8Yy6Z4?t=228): Unanchored destruction needs every block welded to its neighbours; welding hundreds of parts manually is too slow, so script it. Keep the algorithm fast or run it offline.
- 2024-11-04 SmartyRBX [1:15](https://youtu.be/Pwy9U0B8tJk?t=75): Pick one skill (scripting, building, modeling, UI, animation) and make lots of small creations. Skill compounds fastest with focused repetition.
- 2024-11-04 SmartyRBX [1:57](https://youtu.be/Pwy9U0B8tJk?t=117): Once small projects work, combine skills into bigger games. Scripting in particular can lead to full games.
- 2024-11-04 SmartyRBX [30:50](https://youtu.be/Pwy9U0B8tJk?t=1850): Make each release tangibly better than the last. Speaker says most creators think they improve but do not.
- 2024-11-08 PineappleDev [0:00](https://youtu.be/Bgdqc4uAjtw?t=0): Stop relying on tutorials by taking a tutorial you followed and extending it with a small addition rather than starting from scratch.
- 2024-11-08 PineappleDev [0:40](https://youtu.be/Bgdqc4uAjtw?t=40): Break each feature into the smallest possible steps (e.g., get sound, store it in a variable, play it when checkpoint is touched).
- 2024-11-08 PineappleDev [2:44](https://youtu.be/Bgdqc4uAjtw?t=164): Recreate existing game mechanics (e.g., speed coil, gravity coil) one-for-one to learn how they work, then build your own.
- 2024-11-08 PineappleDev [3:25](https://youtu.be/Bgdqc4uAjtw?t=205): Write a numbered list of steps before building a feature, e.g., get models, get spawn positions, clone model, place at position.
- 2024-11-28 Suphi Kaner [0:58](https://youtu.be/SVLFkdeh6c8?t=58): Use the Global Library model from the Toolbox as a starting point; the framework does not force a particular programming style.
- 2024-12-01 SmartyRBX [5:24](https://youtu.be/_-LugzuCA38?t=324): When blocked, adapt: use free assets, learn Blender, Google the problem, watch a video, ask on the DevForum, or rework the feature.
- 2024-12-01 SmartyRBX [7:04](https://youtu.be/_-LugzuCA38?t=424): Start small and finish the project even if imperfect; every finished project teaches lessons for the next one.
- 2024-12-01 SmartyRBX [7:36](https://youtu.be/_-LugzuCA38?t=456): Build, break, and learn on every project; small consistent steps compound more than a single giant leap.
- 2024-12-12 VuukStudios [14:52](https://youtu.be/xBPJnMgLoUE?t=892): Batch-record videos and pre-record a week's uploads to run multiple channels with few hours per day.
- 2025-01-05 janisjanis01 [14:53](https://youtu.be/EpPU3Am8lgE?t=893): Keep adding content until progression feels balanced; creator found five bags insufficient and added three more.
- 2025-01-10 PineappleDev [15:02](https://youtu.be/hHKQEohHqwU?t=902): Expose all feel parameters (bob speed, distance, FOV, lean, smoothing, sensitivity, unlock key) as top-level variables so they can be tuned or moved to a ModuleScript.
- 2025-01-15 CallMeDogey [5:58](https://youtu.be/USJC5l_wpuY?t=358): Update carefully: bugs after updates caused outages; test updates before pushing, since he was up overnight fixing them.
- 2025-01-24 ByteBlox [24:50](https://youtu.be/SCtYVCRVzM8?t=1490): Use AI to draft scaffolding, but expect to debug it heavily; the speaker judged manual scripting faster than repeatedly prompting fixes.
- 2025-01-29 JustDucksey [5:36](https://youtu.be/jKyzG9HPrRM?t=336): Keep the first version simple and scoped; the dev cut seeds to speed progress and built features in small playable steps.
- 2025-02-04 GameMakers [9:45](https://youtu.be/qZRNpN5M8jA?t=585): Before soft launch, maximise learning per dollar spent. Confirm the game has traction before raising more money or spending on UA.
- 2025-02-07 Duckable [0:21](https://youtu.be/OS-k2iOEfhM?t=21): A solo developer built the game in about 4 months, showing a small scoped MVP can ship fast.
- 2025-02-14 Duckable [1:15](https://youtu.be/6g8ZYtDKPBw?t=75): Study DevForum technique posts before coding unfamiliar systems like placement; the speaker spent about 10 hours on the placement system.
- 2025-03-02 SmartyRBX [1:54](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=114): Master one dev skill (e.g. scripting) before learning others; splitting focus across many skills slowed the speaker's progress.
- 2025-03-02 SmartyRBX [23:07](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1387): Prioritise results (players and Robux) over passion projects; release simple games in days or a week rather than months.
- 2025-03-02 SmartyRBX [24:53](https://youtu.be/fz-0n9ed9xU?t=1493): Monetise early releases and reinvest earnings to fund bigger projects and hire developers later.
- 2025-03-09 BloxxedDev [0:00](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=0): Set a hard time limit and scope to core mechanics first; the creator cut the ending and polish short to protect the time budget.
- 2025-03-09 BloxxedDev [1:09](https://youtu.be/CipKM4DBWxg?t=69): Drop multiplayer for a small prototype; the creator avoided it to skip networking problems.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [12:36](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=756): Plan features before polishing areas; missing features forced rebuilding the gym area late.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [13:38](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=818): Gloves per world multiply fast: 4 designs x 4 variants (incl. golden and charged) = 16 per world; scope item counts before starting.
- 2025-03-09 janisjanis01 [18:35](https://youtu.be/EJ8qN7EKafM?t=1115): Save Blender work frequently; crashes and lost connections wiped out modelling progress.
- 2025-03-21 PineappleDev [0:19](https://youtu.be/E6Bev4VCWeA?t=19): Start from the provided model kit and place folders in ReplicatedStorage, ServerScriptService and StarterPlayerScripts as instructed to get a working drag system quickly.
- 2025-03-22 AlvinBlox [2:36](https://youtu.be/dtRGRx-2WNg?t=156): Simple, well-executed mechanics tend to win on Roblox; avoid overcomplicated designs.
- 2025-03-22 CallMeDogey [0:00](https://youtu.be/rHyiN2GV-J0?t=0): Split roles: builder makes environments and lobby while a coder implements matchmaking and systems in parallel, enabling fast daily progress.
- 2025-03-22 CallMeDogey [9:29](https://youtu.be/rHyiN2GV-J0?t=569): Ship a scoped-down playable version in a fixed deadline; accept scuffed systems and iterate post-release based on player feedback.
- 2025-03-23 SmartyRBX [4:24](https://youtu.be/_wqZyaTEsHQ?t=264): Launch an MVP: the most barebones version that is already fun, then update based on player feedback.
- 2025-03-30 janisjanis01 [0:00](https://youtu.be/Pruto1wqxAU?t=0): Pick event-style updates (e.g. seasonal Easter event) to ship fresh content faster than building a whole new world.
- 2025-04-01 PineappleDev [1:29](https://youtu.be/KTS-9A9DLvY?t=89): Before customizing, verify the queue works end-to-end: publish the lobby and destination places, set both to public, then test a teleport.
- 2025-04-04 AlvinBlox [6:37](https://youtu.be/f63stwpsaXw?t=397): Start with one core idea and avoid feature creep; add features later as updates based on feedback.
- 2025-04-11 PineappleDev [0:14](https://youtu.be/kVtEBqvw5F4?t=14): Install the item drag/weld system first; the buy/sell system depends on the carry weld system from the previous tutorial.
- 2025-04-11 PineappleDev [6:37](https://youtu.be/kVtEBqvw5F4?t=397): When adding a new item, call the item setup function with its data and object so it works with the buy/sell system.
- 2025-04-11 PineappleDev [6:37](https://youtu.be/kVtEBqvw5F4?t=397): Duplicate buy tables to add more shops; they work immediately when the purchase name and shop data are correct.
- 2025-04-19 JustDucksey [0:47](https://youtu.be/qxE7JhikvwY?t=47): Don't just watch tutorials; after each lesson, build something small with what you learned to lock in the skill.
- 2025-04-19 JustDucksey [2:50](https://youtu.be/qxE7JhikvwY?t=170): Remaking and improving earlier projects is a cheap way to practise and see how much your skills have grown.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [3:45](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=225): Start with a basic idea and a tight loop with few mechanics; test with friends and watch them play before adding features.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [9:33](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=573): Ship a playable MVP quickly, gather feedback, then iterate; don't spend months perfecting an idea before release.
- 2025-04-28 AlvinBlox [9:33](https://youtu.be/Le4QhC3dDGk?t=573): Ship a testable MVP quickly, gather feedback, then update; don't spend six months perfecting an idea before release.
- 2025-05-02 Duckable [0:00](https://youtu.be/Wc8wjiy1oYk?t=0): Scope aggressively for short builds: map building took the speaker 2+ hours, and the trolling system took 4, so cut features that aren't core to the game.
- 2025-05-03 LovJak [0:50](https://youtu.be/M-o5hLZbjGY?t=50): Break problems into small parts and solve one at a time; add effects, damage or rewards layer by layer after each piece works.
- 2025-05-07 JustDucksey [0:00](https://youtu.be/o-8KX6XgC7s?t=0): Study the original game first (shops, plots, map) and write a checklist of main systems before building.
- 2025-05-07 JustDucksey [5:29](https://youtu.be/o-8KX6XgC7s?t=329): Use a friend's growth system module to parallelise work and split tasks across a team.
- 2025-05-19 Leif [0:26](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=26): Use version control (Git/GitHub) from the start; Studio undo doesn't reliably restore changes and collaboration via copy-paste is error-prone.
- 2025-05-19 Leif [0:26](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=26): Use Git version control even when working solo; commits act as restore points and undo in Studio does not reliably recover changes.
- 2025-05-19 Leif [9:20](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=560): Manage shared libraries (e.g. ProfileService, Promise, React, Fusion) with Wally instead of copying them manually; put server-only packages in a server dependency section.
- 2025-05-19 Leif [11:55](https://youtu.be/IJDg6tRJmHo?t=715): Add Wally package folders and autogenerated files (sourcemap.json, wally.lock) to .gitignore so Git tracks only your own code.
- 2025-05-23 RoDev [4:43](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=283): Dev 2 spent about 2 hours on core money and save scripts; scope each session to one system so progress is testable.
- 2025-05-23 RoDev [15:29](https://youtu.be/Gw2FwndwnOg?t=929): Use a clear hand-off list between devs; the video shows the cost of unplanned changes to an existing codebase.
- 2025-06-07 AlvinBlox [10:30](https://youtu.be/Y93DRfB-Deo?t=630): Build passion projects and small commissions to develop skills while lacking budget; avoid going all-in on one project early.
- 2025-06-09 BubuTheDev [6:30](https://youtu.be/0UYtSJvYe4U?t=390): Organize the workspace into folders (Map, scripts, lighting) and name models descriptively (e.g. 'stairs', 'ladder') so the explorer stays navigable for updates.
- 2025-06-14 JustDucksey [0:42](https://youtu.be/XRvcKGSLr4g?t=42): Reuse free Roblox assets (e.g. the free zombie NPCs) instead of building your own when a prototype needs placeholders; it saves time.
- 2025-06-14 Crusherfire [24:31](https://youtu.be/oUjrs6Apd7A?t=1471): Make it work first, then make it right, then make it fast; don't over-optimize closures before a game or system is complete.
- 2025-06-15 janisjanis01 [3:34](https://youtu.be/W9JYImC0FBg?t=214): Use placeholder items and prototypes to test core mechanics early, then build real content once the core loop works.
- 2025-06-22 SmartyRBX [6:13](https://youtu.be/BMS5iYw_8Vk?t=373): Learn scripting as a high-leverage skill so you can turn a map into a playable game without hiring a scripter, which can be expensive.
- 2025-06-22 SmartyRBX [12:56](https://youtu.be/BMS5iYw_8Vk?t=776): Monetize a skill through simple, smaller deliverables (commissions, assets, courses) before attempting a full game.
- 2025-06-23 SmartyRBX [2:21](https://youtu.be/tpVRSnWwZWI?t=141): Pick one core skill (scripting is the speaker's recommended default) and test several via tutorials in Studio before committing to one.
- 2025-06-23 SmartyRBX [13:41](https://youtu.be/tpVRSnWwZWI?t=821): Make simple, easy-to-understand games first (simulators, hot potato, freeze tag) that you can finish, then level up complexity.
- 2025-06-23 SmartyRBX [14:22](https://youtu.be/tpVRSnWwZWI?t=862): Monetize simple games early to fund hiring of other devs for scripting, building, UI, and art.
- 2025-06-24 SmartyRBX [9:31](https://youtu.be/skcp4P68i84?t=571): Make simple games first (e.g. hot potato, freeze tag), release them fast as MVPs, and increase complexity as skill and funds grow.
- 2025-06-24 SmartyRBX [15:29](https://youtu.be/skcp4P68i84?t=929): Keep project scope small at the start; a dream game with too large a scope often never ships.
- 2025-06-26 Mulch [8:13](https://youtu.be/B2vC4-QArWg?t=493): Commissioning a working queue system after own attempt failed; outsource systems you can't get working reliably.
- 2025-06-28 SmartyRBX [10:03](https://youtu.be/Lplz7ZFcKlQ?t=603): Make simple games first as a career step; a monetised simple game can earn 10-20x a builder's monthly commission income if it retains players.
- 2025-06-29 SmartyRBX [3:09](https://youtu.be/MAnvGNgBKgQ?t=189): Start with simple games like hot potato, an obby, or a simulator instead of your dream game.
- 2025-06-29 SmartyRBX [8:07](https://youtu.be/MAnvGNgBKgQ?t=487): Ship imperfect games early and iterate using market feedback rather than polishing for months.
- 2025-07-04 SmartyRBX [8:00](https://youtu.be/ddfqOQ7H_h8?t=480): Ship simple games first to test in the market and iterate on real feedback instead of spending 6 months to 3 years on a dream game.
- 2025-07-06 SmartyRBX [2:29](https://youtu.be/Gml4to_jXQ0?t=149): Specialize in one core skill first; scripting is the most profitable in-Studio skill because it lets you finish and release full games.
- 2025-07-06 SmartyRBX [3:52](https://youtu.be/Gml4to_jXQ0?t=232): If unsure which skill to learn, pick one at random and commit to it rather than stalling.
- 2025-07-06 SmartyRBX [17:33](https://youtu.be/Gml4to_jXQ0?t=1053): Make simple, proven-format games (hide and seek, obby, tycoon-style simulator, freeze tag) first to get published and learn from player feedback.
- 2025-07-09 SmartyRBX [4:06](https://youtu.be/vBllyv2fnWw?t=246): Make simple games first, especially within trends; they are easier to produce and appeal to the young mobile-heavy Roblox audience.
- 2025-07-09 SmartyRBX [5:07](https://youtu.be/vBllyv2fnWw?t=307): Start a new game by building a reusable template first.
- 2025-07-13 janisjanis01 [8:39](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=519): Swap a buggy custom system for a tested open-source module (Snatchel backpack) when bugs pile up, instead of fixing it yourself.
- 2025-07-13 janisjanis01 [8:39](https://youtu.be/pD8fKkzF8O8?t=519): Reuse your own systems from earlier games (plot assignment, hotbar, tutorial, shop) to save time.
- 2025-07-17 Roblox [6:18](https://youtu.be/ckNAwhl-n0g?t=378): Write policy as simple, non-redundant rules with the fewest distinctions needed; complex rules fail at scale for moderators and models.
- 2025-07-21 SmartyRBX [10:40](https://youtu.be/exOJsFcer1Q?t=640): Avoid starting with a complex game as a new dev; scope down to avoid burnout from abandoning large projects.
- 2025-07-25 ArTheDev [1:23](https://youtu.be/Pi8rQ9gGTKE?t=83): Organise combat code by skill set folders (e.g. base, sword) so swapping weapons only changes a combat-type StringValue on the player.
- 2025-07-27 janisjanis01 [5:54](https://youtu.be/Sgs36l6K5EU?t=354): Reuse scripts and systems from previous games to cut build time; creator copied shop, settings and gifting systems.
- 2025-08-00 GamesBeat [page](https://gamesbeat.com/?p=305182): Build simple prototypes fast; the original was made in a few days by a 16-year-old and it was then expanded with a partner studio.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [8:23](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=503): Keep a hidden 'backup' collection and save iterations (necklace_101, 102, 103). Duplicate items before applying modifiers so you can recover them later.
- 2025-08-01 Roblox Creators [1:05:32](https://youtu.be/D64IOXAb6SM?t=3932): Save an unpublished place file and upload textures as Decals via create.roblox.com, then copy the asset ID into the mesh TextureID. Re-export after UV changes or the texture will not match.
- 2025-08-04 waveshine [4:02](https://youtu.be/xnlaYLnCtFc?t=242): Expect to spend hours debugging issues that work in Studio but break on live servers; test in live server conditions before publishing.
- 2025-08-08 bubDEV [2:27](https://youtu.be/Iv80MDXZBXU?t=147): Build the game as a reusable template so a new game can be re-skinned and released in under a day.
- 2025-08-08 bubDEV [2:53](https://youtu.be/Iv80MDXZBXU?t=173): Make the template drag-and-drop so theme swaps (characters, shop, index) need minimal code changes.
- 2025-08-15 Roblox Creators [4:11](https://youtu.be/KSez8Ecq50A?t=251): Build a catalog of roughly 25 items with variety before treating it as a business; Rush says he has a couple hundred now.
- 2025-08-24 janisjanis01 [13:06](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=786): Batch-film footage and slice it in one session; the creator cut editing time from ~1 hour to ~13 minutes by staying focused.
- 2025-08-24 janisjanis01 [13:06](https://youtu.be/5iIIYKztamo?t=786): Hiring an editor freed up time while keeping output; outsourcing editing is a viable way to sustain daily posting.
- 2025-08-29 Leif [1:00](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=60): Organize code into a shared/services folder with one folder per service; keep server versions under ServerScriptService so exploiters cannot download sensitive code.
- 2025-08-29 Leif [0:29](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=29): Split game logic into services that each own one slice (e.g. BuildService, EnemyService), with client/server versions and shared utilities in a utility module.
- 2025-08-29 Leif [9:32](https://youtu.be/F3ASTJuO82A?t=572): Reuse dependency-free packages across projects (Signal, Charm, Sift, Data service, Janitor) and manage versions with Wally rather than reinventing them.
- 2025-09-05 Roblox [42:52](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=2572): Use Studio's one-click multi-platform deploy (iOS, Android, Mac, PC, console, VR) to ship a new version in under 30 minutes.
- 2025-09-05 Roblox [23:26](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=1406): Use Roblox native tools (Studio with drafts) for maximum velocity; minimize external IDEs and tooling to cut friction.
