- 2025-09-05 Roblox [23:26](https://youtu.be/6iB5__RCQDc?t=1406): Enable drafts (branching) once team grows beyond ~10 engineers so one buggy change doesn't block everyone.
- 2025-09-07 janisjanis01 [0:17](https://youtu.be/YVKHlrlWn-k?t=17): Plugging a third-party framework into an existing game required rewriting most of its scripts; budget time for that rework.
- 2025-09-09 SmartyRBX [6:31](https://youtu.be/ysqeKxPqBXA?t=391): Build a repeatable system of funding, project management, accountability and hiring so shipping no longer depends on your own studio work.
- 2025-09-13 bubDEV [2:49](https://youtu.be/kg_h09_x7no?t=169): Expect the first winning concept to take months of iteration; improve with each game rather than hoping for a quick hit.
- 2025-09-21 janisjanis01 [4:26](https://youtu.be/uNLDi_GO2MA?t=266): Simplify core mechanics: creator cut carrying crops to a base in favour of direct inventory collection to keep gameplay simpler.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [10:12](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=612): After a viral launch, list problems to fix immediately, list player-requested directions, and pick updates at the intersection with what you want to build.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [34:48](https://youtu.be/YgyfcMfxQcI?t=2088): Schedule sustainable hours and plan breaks to prevent burnout; constrained daily work sessions can keep the project fun.
- 2025-09-26 Erthlord [5:25](https://youtu.be/fdDscQ6CWIs?t=325): Trying to ship a trend-chasing game in 7 days was too tight; the creator needed about 9 days and scope still slipped.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [2:11](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=131): Ship risky features behind runtime config feature flags so you can instantly disable them without hotfix or server restart.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [13:29](https://youtu.be/j6OPA95-1O0?t=809): Start high-risk features at small group sizes (10% in Brookhaven's avatar editor case) and ramp after confidence builds.
- 2025-09-26 Roblox [19:47](https://youtu.be/rwLEnBtXr30?t=1187): Build debug tools to spawn load in Studio so you can reproduce performance problems before release.
- 2025-09-27 SimTek Game Development [28:19](https://youtu.be/EQqd8Xk3Wkk?t=1699): Use rename-symbol refactoring in Studio to rename a variable across a script when the name changes, instead of manual find and replace.
- 2025-10-09 JustDucksey [0:41](https://youtu.be/XZKHQPObHgw?t=41): In a 48-hour jam, pick one simple core concept (a button and wandering enemies) and build it first, then layer on extras.
- 2025-10-18 Creators on the Blox [35:48](https://youtu.be/WODeLtB2YH4?t=2148): Write the script first, read it aloud to the team, and only start production after it is green-lit; this filters weak ideas before costly building.
- 2025-10-18 Creators on the Blox [1:04:02](https://youtu.be/WODeLtB2YH4?t=3842): Build skills on a paid project for another channel (he wrote and directed Brookhaven stories) before applying them to your own channel.
- 2025-10-25 Creators on the Blox [22:18](https://youtu.be/XtWTnz56ZpM?t=1338): Weekly floor-based updates with 3-4 people is a heavy load; sustained weekly cadence for months is hard, so scope updates realistically.
- 2025-10-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940): Recognise when an idea isn't working and move on quickly rather than holding onto it.
- 2025-10-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940): Designer and programmer scale systems together: build a small piece, then push it to thousands of entities until something breaks, then iterate.
- 2025-10-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940): Use a fast build process: the team's 3-month build was far faster than their previous 6-month projects, and speed allowed iteration.
- 2025-11-05 Possible Panda [1:05](https://youtu.be/L-2GA_xX-SA?t=65): Install Iris by dropping the release .rbxm into ReplicatedStorage/packages, or add it via Wally to keep it updated.
- 2025-11-06 VuukStudios [3:03](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=183): Keep early games to about 2-4 weeks of work; release unfinished games and iterate rather than polishing for months.
- 2025-11-06 VuukStudios [5:54](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=354): Organise each game in Trello with columns for concept, systems, monetisation, building/art, and paid costs.
- 2025-11-06 VuukStudios [16:08](https://youtu.be/AbjI7ygMnao?t=968): Reuse a working game source template to release reskinned games quickly once one concept is proven.
- 2025-11-07 DitchyDevelopments [1:27](https://youtu.be/3Pa8QmFB_TE?t=87): Taking feature requests from short-form comments led to adding an obby and a race mode, which expanded scope fast; he later said it probably wasn't a good idea.
- 2025-11-07 Possible Panda [1:08](https://youtu.be/qo0x-Et5NPk?t=68): Install UI Labs from the Roblox marketplace to preview UI components without launching the game, speeding up UI iteration.
- 2025-11-07 Possible Panda [8:11](https://youtu.be/qo0x-Et5NPk?t=491): Group stories into a storybook ModuleScript with name and storyRoots set to script so related components appear together.
- 2025-11-10 Creators on the Blox [31:07](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=1867): Make a small game you can beat in one sitting first. Zeal says jumping straight into a huge project made Pressure too big for its own good.
- 2025-11-10 Creators on the Blox [34:29](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=2069): Plan a rework with reuse in mind: Zeal's overhaul reuses existing enemies and systems with small changes to cut workload.
- 2025-11-10 Creators on the Blox [33:49](https://youtu.be/Qn0uVUSxG8I?t=2029): Cancel an event that is not ready rather than burn out the team; Zeal shelved a 30%-complete Halloween event.
- 2025-11-15 Creators on the Blox [48:08](https://youtu.be/W8ghgtipQgI?t=2888): Expect to learn project management fast when hiring devs: use ClickUp, daily calls and detailed specs, since the team underdelivered without close management.
- 2025-11-15 Creators on the Blox [1:36:57](https://youtu.be/W8ghgtipQgI?t=5817): Short-form content now outperforms long-form for many Roblox audiences; test shorts, live streams and long-form together to find what works.
- 2025-11-21 Duckable [0:00](https://youtu.be/F9sfnpkzcPw?t=0): Scope a 24-hour build tightly; the speaker's overrun came from rewrites, polish and map work, and the deadline was missed.
- 2025-11-23 janisjanis01 [11:19](https://youtu.be/iEvgIId-8Uw?t=679): Avoid rushing release; the creator said rushing caused the game's multiple problems.
- 2025-11-28 VuukStudios [15:44](https://youtu.be/U4nduRcszJ4?t=944): Start with the Zero to Hero scripting course if aiming to become a scripter; it covers foundations for other tracks.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [1:04](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=64): Wrap game values (shop text, colors, features, event toggles) in configs so you can change them live without redeploying servers.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [13:51](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=831): Keep config keys composable (e.g. checkout.buyButton.Text vs buttonText) so configs stay organised as they grow.
- 2025-12-01 Roblox Creators [20:18](https://youtu.be/-CdXP025MvU?t=1218): Avoid running overlapping experiments on the same features since they can interact and skew results.
- 2025-12-01 Matt The Method [0:35](https://youtu.be/MPHPumDvMIs?t=35): Build a simple, easy-to-ship tycoon/simulator; creator spent ~40 hours initially plus ~20 hours since, prioritising a traffic funnel over design polish.
- 2025-12-03 Lnor Roblox [4:40](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=280): Plan the big update as a set of systems before coding. Rewriting the item system took months, so design data structures for the whole game up front.
- 2025-12-03 Lnor Roblox [6:11](https://youtu.be/Nywo2NwQPNo?t=371): Expect the final stretch to slip. Estimates of 'next week' stretched by months, with a month spent on one exploit (pickaxes mining ores).
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157): Enable Server Authority in Studio via File > Beta Features, then set NextGenerationReplication, UseFixedSimulation, StreamingEnabled, PlayerScriptsUseInputActionSystem on, SignalBehavior to Deferred, then AuthorityMode to Server.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157): Start by enabling only Workspace.AuthorityMode for simple default-avatar physics; complex custom gameplay needs adapting scripts to BindToSimulation and InputAction.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157): Reference the official example places (Laser Tag, Soccer, Racing) and the Server Authority docs to learn the pattern before adopting it.
- 2025-12-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321): Report API gaps with a detailed use-case description and repro; the team explicitly asks for this feedback before the early 2026 launch.
- 2025-12-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-requirements-to-publish-and-update-public-experiences/4143953): Build and playtest privately with teammates; private experiences have no eligibility requirement for playtesters or editors.
- 2025-12-13 janisjanis01 [4:00](https://youtu.be/JYvGdKRzwEo?t=240): Build the core loop first (buttons, droppers, ATM, save/load) before art; the creator got the base mechanics working by day two.
- 2025-12-15 SimTek Game Development [0:00](https://youtu.be/023CG-y2xPY?t=0): Build incrementally: add one feature per video and test each step in Studio before moving on, with a print statement to confirm code paths fire.
- 2025-12-18 Ventures [5:46](https://youtu.be/Nb50UhXcpRM?t=346): Hit Roblox games can be built in 8-10 weeks using Roblox Studio tooling. Fast iteration is a competitive advantage.
- 2025-12-21 Daiorg [4:38](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=278): Learn from free resources: Roblox Developer Hub, YouTube tutorials, and reading well-made open-source games; escalate to a friend or AI when stuck.
- 2025-12-21 Daiorg [44:05](https://youtu.be/JCMIb008Y6E?t=2645): Plan only the first ~20 minutes of gameplay in detail and leave the rest open-ended, then fill mid and late game from player data after launch.
- 2025-12-24 1_Bruce [9:47](https://youtu.be/wKI3PBO3bB0?t=587): Keep initial development cost near zero (he spent under 50k Robux or nothing) and only spend Robux after a game proves it is doing well.
- 2025-12-24 1_Bruce [0:45](https://youtu.be/wKI3PBO3bB0?t=45): Short-lived games built in about a month can earn fast revenue; he says these last 1-2 months, so plan for quick releases.
- 2025-12-25 Creators on the Blox [1:30:07](https://youtu.be/LyJ04iE6K1M?t=5407): Work around limited hardware; his early party games were built on a bad office PC and phone-based Fortnite maps.
- 2025-12-25 Creators on the Blox [19:11](https://youtu.be/LyJ04iE6K1M?t=1151): Many finished games go unreleased because they feel weak; he now only ships games that meet a higher bar.
- 2026-01-03 Leif [6:00](https://youtu.be/Ezk3vEOvv8o?t=360): Use the UI Labs plugin with story scripts to see and edit React UI in real time, without playing the game for each change.
- 2026-01-05 ItsZyonic [3:25](https://youtu.be/4-ge-E9WvM4?t=205): Expect solo development to take about twice as long as planned; build in buffer before release dates.
- 2026-01-09 TheDankDeveloper [8:03](https://youtu.be/BYhhm9o3H_E?t=483): Work in timed prompt sprints to see how many features an AI tool ships; review each output before the next prompt.
- 2026-01-26 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561): Enable StarterPlayer.AvatarJointUpgrade now to test breaking changes early; Phase 1 keeps the default Disabled with no breaking change.
- 2026-01-27 TheDankDeveloper [10:56](https://youtu.be/3y7EU49_d7s?t=656): Expect the final prompt to introduce regressions; reserve time to verify core loops (forging, selling) after each change.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Start prototypes from the Avatar Abilities Beta Template placefile to test default abilities before integrating into your own experience.
- 2026-02-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904): Expect breaking API changes during beta; they are limited to once a week and announced a day in advance with change notes.
- 2026-02-25 Roblox [0:43](https://youtu.be/8Djgx0jEyfs?t=43): Build functional objects, not just meshes: generated objects need axles, motors, and scripts so behaviours like doors or helicopters work in-engine.
- 2026-03-09 GamesBeat [page](https://gamesbeat.com/roblox-unveils-jumpstart-and-incubator-programs-to-help-creators-expand-possibilities-exclusive/): Studio's shared-environment collaboration and built-in cloud version control reduce lost work and friction when iterating.
- 2026-03-14 Creators on the Blox [48:11](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=2891): Make sure the game is fun before adding content; Doors' first test was a white hallway with two monsters, and it was fun, so they kept building.
- 2026-03-14 Creators on the Blox [19:19](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=1159): Plan the full scope before building a sequel floor; Floor 2 was built under pressure with many cut features due to poor upfront planning.
- 2026-03-14 Creators on the Blox [11:43](https://youtu.be/VyBSvWSIkao?t=703): Expect to rebuild key builds several times; Redibles rebuilt the Doors builds about three times before settling on a version.
- 2026-03-15 1_Bruce [1:16](https://youtu.be/zgq6L4ZzCHs?t=76): Keep scripting clean so updates are feasible; speaker says hard-to-update scripting hurt update cadence, though retention stayed solid.
- 2026-03-18 VuukStudios [1:05](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=65): Learn scripting first; the speaker says it is about 90% of a game and the biggest learning curve.
- 2026-03-18 VuukStudios [16:06](https://youtu.be/ba1raV5HE-c?t=966): Spend 2-3 hours a day in Studio practising buttons and simple maps; the speaker suggests more.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [12:26](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=746): Ship a working 'it works' solution for one-time events; over-engineering the system (Halloween Spotlight) left no time to make the actual content.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [40:56](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=2456): Scope events conservatively. Jasper's ambitious Halloween Spotlight scope caused stress and a weaker reception than the Hunt Mega Edition.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [1:04:13](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=3853): Make all animation/cutscene pacing first, then have voice actors record to it; dialogue animation done after voice work forces rushing or dragging.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [1:04:56](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=3896): Build the framework and groundwork first, then polish details on top. Make things exist before making them perfect.
- 2026-03-27 Creators on the Blox [1:06:20](https://youtu.be/LWaw0KNwxyM?t=3980): Cut scope to something achievable but memorable, and leave slots for future additions rather than promising everything in the first pass.
- 2026-03-31 ArTheDev [3:55](https://youtu.be/R3q6hZs8FDE?t=235): Don't start by building a full game as a beginner; build small systems like a shop, door system, or basic ability first.
- 2026-04-02 Roblox [0:44](https://youtu.be/IuJWzpvpIa8?t=44): When early numbers and fun are poor, change the game rather than iterating on a failing version; Rivals was reworked after launch with only a few hundred players.
- 2026-04-06 Deconstructor of Fun [14:40](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=880): Ship a bite-size MVP in 5-6 weeks instead of 3-4 months, then live-op weekly with the community based on player feedback.
- 2026-04-06 Deconstructor of Fun [21:02](https://youtu.be/PNeLRjewQns?t=1262): Iterate many times a day. Survive Bikini Bottom shipped 10 builds in 3 days with no app-store review step.
- 2026-04-06 ArTheDev [0:00](https://youtu.be/RDjsR4iIRlg?t=0): Structure inventory as a reusable module (add/remove/change functions) instead of one script so it can be reused across genres and extended later.
- 2026-04-08 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267): Build new projects on the Character Controller Library now; enabling it later means refactoring character logic when Crouch and Sprint land.
- 2026-04-24 SimTek Game Development [2:04](https://youtu.be/XBi5o4JJNsw?t=124): Start from a template like Baseplate to skip setup and get a working test scene immediately.
- 2026-04-24 SimTek Game Development [4:47](https://youtu.be/XBi5o4JJNsw?t=287): Publish to Roblox early so the game exists in the Creator Hub; new places start private.
- 2026-05-01 LovJak [0:41](https://youtu.be/FpbZRgRHYjQ?t=41): Start from a professional template to save build time; templates include instructions for changing things, so fixing animations and swapping assets is quick.
- 2026-05-01 Duckable [2:17](https://youtu.be/X9YYjb_mY5w?t=137): Use AI for mundane coding tasks but review output; he found it fast for scripts but error-prone for inventory and needed manual fixes.
- 2026-05-03 rasdev [2:37](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=157): Build an MVP: one map, one core loop, no extras. Release it, then iterate based on real player feedback rather than building a large game first.
- 2026-05-03 rasdev [3:18](https://youtu.be/q0lIbkUC2r8?t=198): Prioritise speed over polish and bug-free code at the early stage; a finished simple game beats an unfinished complex one.
- 2026-05-04 ArTheDev [0:24](https://youtu.be/3FB6ZzAON8I?t=24): Build small systems instead of huge ideas; scope down so each piece can be finished and learned from.
- 2026-05-04 ArTheDev [2:41](https://youtu.be/3FB6ZzAON8I?t=161): Use tutorials for basics only; then build on your own so you learn to think independently rather than follow steps.
- 2026-05-07 rasdev [1:21](https://youtu.be/wGRgITzP18c?t=81): Don't keep making small title, icon, and description tweaks on a weak game idea; the core idea must be strong first.
- 2026-05-07 rasdev [4:04](https://youtu.be/wGRgITzP18c?t=244): Build the core loop before adding features, and package the game (title, icon) before finishing it.
- 2026-05-09 Creators on the Blox [28:48](https://youtu.be/JsiEVtWLwnA?t=1728): Avoid overthinking and designing for imagined fan reactions; Part 2 was over-engineered and slower than the simpler, more natural Part 1.
- 2026-05-09 Creators on the Blox [30:11](https://youtu.be/JsiEVtWLwnA?t=1811): Don't depend on one scripter; when scripters leave, the project stalls. Keep backup scripters lined up.
- 2026-05-10 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258): Don't optimize prematurely; optimize when the expected time saving is meaningful (e.g. 20s to 5s load) or the code runs constantly.
- 2026-05-12 Yann [0:00](https://youtu.be/dbNA3K8lt2w?t=0): Build small, proven-loop games fast: the creator made Grow Bamboo in about one week by copying a working core loop.
- 2026-05-16 Tizzy [16:02](https://youtu.be/inWzxY26NXo?t=962): Loop: run ads, find your worst stat (e.g. D1), ship a meaningful update fixing it, then run ads again for fresh initial data.
- 2026-05-18 rasdev [5:52](https://youtu.be/N3L63uyLyQ8?t=352): Scope the first release as a minimal MVP; the speaker's example is an arena, basic stat progression, simple currency and a leaderboard, built in 2-4 weeks.
- 2026-05-19 MoseyTube [3:24](https://youtu.be/400F-3qXoHE?t=204): Build iteratively: keep a back-and-forth chat with Claude, asking for fixes when something looks wrong, until the game is done.
- 2026-05-26 Tizzy [8:12](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=492): Scope an MVP first: just the core loop and basic progression, then playtest to check whether the game is fun.
- 2026-05-26 Tizzy [10:58](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=658): Write a game design document covering game, player actions, leveling, economy and progression; use it as the scope and SOW basis.
- 2026-05-26 Tizzy [11:38](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=698): Break the design doc into Trello columns per discipline (programmer, builder, UI) with explicit task details.
- 2026-05-26 Tizzy [13:00](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=780): Finish models, builds and UI before bringing in the programmer; a pre-built world is more motivating to code into.
- 2026-05-26 Tizzy [14:23](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=863): Launch the MVP and gather live player data quickly rather than overthinking; playtesters can be used before going live.
- 2026-05-26 Tizzy [16:27](https://youtu.be/7tYPvXYlif8?t=987): Stick to the agreed scope; send new ideas as new contracts to avoid resentment and bogged-down developers.
- 2026-05-27 Roblox [0:44](https://youtu.be/-14vhiLL4xE?t=44): Scrap slow projects and ship something small and fast; he dropped a months-long project to build Dead Rails quickly, then posted and sponsored it on Roblox.
- 2026-05-30 Yann [7:30](https://youtu.be/yw32DZtz4mQ?t=450): From day 5, stop ads if benchmarks and core loop are weak; keep running them if benchmarks are good until home recommendations appear.
- 2026-05-31 Duckable [21:31](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=1291): Use 24-hour build challenges to force scope limits and ship a complete game quickly.
- 2026-05-31 Duckable [13:33](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=813): Abandon ambitious large projects in favour of smaller, simpler games; repeated big-project attempts burned out motivation.
- 2026-05-31 Duckable [12:15](https://youtu.be/52srr65Mksc?t=735): Use official Roblox documentation when stuck rather than relying on AI code generation, which the creator found unreliable.
- 2026-06-04 rasdev [4:05](https://youtu.be/q0uePCbBlk0?t=245): Scope a solo beginner's first game to a 2-4 week MVP: the smallest playable, functional version, with simple mechanics and features not fully polished.
- 2026-06-05 Creators on the Blox [17:39](https://youtu.be/5vJCBrxPHmY?t=1059): Jailbreak went from idea (Dec 2016) to April 2017 release in about four months, built as a passion project alongside full-time jobs.
- 2026-06-06 janisjanis01 [1:22](https://youtu.be/CltiB53QTHw?t=82): Work in a separate, unpublished copy; the creator accidentally deleted their main game mid-build, so back up before destructive edits.
- 2026-06-07 ArTheDev [2:43](https://youtu.be/wG9XzLnJC5k?t=163): Finish and publish small games; a tiny finished game with 12 players teaches more than a massive unfinished one.
- 2026-06-07 ArTheDev [2:43](https://youtu.be/wG9XzLnJC5k?t=163): Pick a game you can finish, e.g. a tiny horror game, simple tycoon, physics toy, or obby with one unique mechanic.
- 2026-06-07 ArTheDev [4:08](https://youtu.be/wG9XzLnJC5k?t=248): Ask 'what game can I actually finish?' rather than 'what game will succeed?'
- 2026-06-09 rasdev [5:28](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=328): Before picking a genre, honestly ask: can I build it with my team size and time? A solo dev cannot ship a full RPG in 3 months.
- 2026-06-09 rasdev [4:14](https://youtu.be/U9RGcuxt9m4?t=254): Scope the MVP to the smallest fully working version with core features, targeting a 2-4 week build for a solo dev.
- 2026-06-10 Tizzy [0:42](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=42): Ship frequent small updates; Hack a Business dev updated daily, sometimes twice a day, and followed the data.
- 2026-06-10 Tizzy [15:33](https://youtu.be/HFcegpPPvpI?t=933): After each update check the metric; if it did not improve, revert and form a new hypothesis.
- 2026-06-19 Yann [2:44](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=164): Scope the project with a clear core loop and a release checklist; decide team, budget, timeline and paid assets upfront.
- 2026-06-19 Yann [3:25](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=205): Assume you will take longer than you think; most developers underestimate how long tasks take.
- 2026-06-19 Yann [3:39](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=219): Build a template place file with your standard structure, modules, configs, lighting and services, and reuse it for every new project.
- 2026-06-19 Yann [5:22](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=322): When stuck or overwhelmed, switch to a different system or task that sounds fun, then return later.
- 2026-06-19 Yann [6:38](https://youtu.be/zWeDpmrjhUM?t=398): Reach a functional MVP first, then add polish: VFX, SFX, particles and animations replace placeholders.
- 2026-06-25 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-unlocking-massive-terrain-with-up-to-64-custom-materials/4703282): Revert the Early Access opt-in in account settings before editing any live game or Team Create place; opted-in edits make the game unplayable for non-program users.
- 2026-06-29 VuukStudios [0:57](https://youtu.be/nvnSmeP3_xM?t=57): Ship a simple trend game fast: the speaker built this one in 4-5 days with one person on maps and one on scripting.
- 2026-06-30 Raul [0:58](https://youtu.be/vnpCimQkHoQ?t=58): Finish and ship updates for a live game with waiting players before starting a new game; avoid abandoning it mid-cycle.
- 2026-07-03 Generalist Programmer [6:04](https://youtu.be/CFohgybeybM?t=364): Run your projected Robux numbers through the DevEx calculator before committing months to a project as a reality check.
- 2026-07-06 Tizzy [4:21](https://youtu.be/EZzztgpSaTU?t=261): Ship updates fast: in the first week of launch, update daily or twice daily, forming hypotheses from data and iterating quickly.
- 2026-07-08 rasdev [4:06](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=246): Release a minimum viable product with the smallest playable feature set as soon as possible, then decide whether to keep building.
- 2026-07-08 rasdev [4:06](https://youtu.be/fPT2GCkK1QM?t=246): Don't wait for the perfect game or moment; it's never worth the delay.
- 2026-07-09 MoseyTube [2:04](https://youtu.be/1Oicl2fFt3o?t=124): Write one detailed master prompt covering income sources, rebirth, UI, monetization and data saving; he got a playable base from it in one shot.
- 2026-07-09 MoseyTube [2:04](https://youtu.be/1Oicl2fFt3o?t=124): Treat AI output as a foundation: the generated games lacked depth (no deep endgame, polish), so add your own systems and polish before launch.
- 2026-07-09 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993): Copy your game and convert the copy rather than editing the live project, since existing games with complex gameplay need script changes.
- 2026-07-14 MoseyTube [5:11](https://youtu.be/-PoyiiGhoNo?t=311): Launch with a basic monetization setup and iterate from sales data and player behaviour rather than trying to perfect it on day one.
- 2026-07-16 Raul [2:26](https://youtu.be/CbAL1e8NgSU?t=146): Show costs above buttons before interaction so players know requirements instantly.
- 2026-07-16 Raul [4:48](https://youtu.be/CbAL1e8NgSU?t=288): Cut scope when a feature is costly; the creator skipped the upgrade tree to ship faster, but this is a tradeoff to weigh against retention.
- 2026-07-19 MoseyTube [2:26](https://youtu.be/PstV93OnOdM?t=146): Expect a large ultra-effort build to take about an hour, and plan to polish placeholder assets and UI before release.
- 2026-07-20 Tizzy [18:22](https://youtu.be/LZOohZHsJus?t=1102): Loop: competitor research, data, idea, ship, check stats, keep iterating on the metric. Ship volume: nine tutorials and daily patches.
- 2026-07-20 rasdev [2:45](https://youtu.be/t3i54NTcDi8?t=165): Spend more time refining the core game idea before building; the speaker argues more idea time correlates with better outcomes.
- 2026-07-23 Yann [6:22](https://youtu.be/Bw2ty2T46z4?t=382): Building a simulator solo in about 3 weeks was feasible, but the creator said the result was not original enough, so scope should include a unique hook.
- 2026-07-27 waveshine [4:53](https://youtu.be/hQ1lrFLEF1c?t=293): Start by building small standalone pieces (shop button, notifications, leaderboard, inventory, save system) and stack them into a game later.
- 2026-07-27 waveshine [5:47](https://youtu.be/hQ1lrFLEF1c?t=347): Use tutorials only for basics, possibilities and specific resources; try first, break things, then search for your exact error.
- 2026-07-27 waveshine [6:28](https://youtu.be/hQ1lrFLEF1c?t=388): Pass the change test: if you can re-price an item or add a second currency without breaking things, you understand the system.
- 2026-07-27 waveshine [8:06](https://youtu.be/hQ1lrFLEF1c?t=486): Expect months to years to learn; keep building and shipping, and accept that most early projects will be scrapped.
- 2026-07-29 VuukStudios [3:07](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=187): Release a base version as soon as the core loop is playable, not a fully finished game, so you can test whether the idea hooks players.
- 2026-07-29 VuukStudios [7:13](https://youtu.be/Bltv__PoNcA?t=433): Work on the game daily, even 30-60 minutes, rather than 7-hour sessions twice a week; daily contact generates more ideas and momentum.
- 2026-07-30 Roblox Creators [35:10](https://youtu.be/yv3GO5AHwJk?t=2110): Put roughly 80% of effort into game development and 20% into marketing; a good game is what converts visibility.
- 2026-08-04 Duckable [1:15](https://youtu.be/Cc7HT3uNjeQ?t=75): Break the game into discrete 'sauce' milestones (plot, tower, droppers, buttons, merge, blender, customers) and finish each before starting the next.
- 2026-08-05 Tizzy [10:47](https://youtu.be/TVdqb9O0RMw?t=647): Run a weekly cycle: plan Saturday after the update, build Mon-Wed, ready Thursday, internal then core playtest Friday, launch Saturday.
- 2026-08-06 VuukStudios [3:23](https://youtu.be/aw0PmBkakb4?t=203): Ship updates almost weekly or biweekly so the game never sits idle; a small team (lead dev plus builders and planners) can sustain that cadence.
- 2026-08-09 studio rook. [1:56](https://youtu.be/EuaV0qc6UXQ?t=116): After a rough playtest, spend the next two weeks fixing most bugs and adding console and mobile support before wider launch.
- 2026-08-09 JustDucksey [4:05](https://youtu.be/kl933X0UCC0?t=245): Speed up growth times during testing so the plant-harvest-sell loop can be verified quickly.
- 2026-08-10 Cumin [18:58](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=1138): Ship a functional core loop early rather than over-engineering for future-proofing; the team spent long on architecture with few resources.
- 2026-08-10 Cumin [4:43](https://youtu.be/xtuuL-wRFho?t=283): Set expectations clearly in beta labels; a beta that is a barebones full release invites backlash.
- 2026-08-18 RoyalDev [0:02](https://youtu.be/gWKwNJRsk3s?t=2): Revisit and rebuild an underperforming game instead of abandoning it; the creator revived an old title with a focused overhaul.
- 2026-08-20 StudioKrew (agency blog, GGAID data) [page](https://studiokrew.com/blog/top-roblox-games-august-2026/): Budget post-launch update cadence as a permanent line item in full-cycle development, not a phase two reached only if launch works.
- 2026-08-21 LovJak [5:51](https://youtu.be/0RZeCn3-XKg?t=351): Plan the full loop (cook, serve, wrong-customer handling, cleanup, table reset) before building; the devlog ran out of time before finishing it.
- 2026-08-21 Business of Creators [9:58](https://youtu.be/lpIzMycpiKo?t=598): Roblox dev pipeline is 4-6 weeks concept to release; traditional studio mindsets (years-long cycles) clash with this platform.
- 2026-08-21 Business of Creators [10:30](https://youtu.be/lpIzMycpiKo?t=630): Get product in front of players fast and learn cheap lessons; perfectionism delays feedback. Good enough beats endlessly polished.
- 2026-08-21 Business of Creators [13:14](https://youtu.be/lpIzMycpiKo?t=794): Launch timing matters: Spongebob shipped just before holidays and only hit the algorithm after about two weeks.
- 2026-08-24 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/x/4828676): Set up access in Creator Dashboard: Credentials/API Keys or OAuth 2.0 App settings, create a permission profile, grant scopes such as universe.analytics:read, then save the key.
- 2026-08-25 MoseyTube [5:41](https://youtu.be/xIBc5He_sy4?t=341): Stay active in the game for the first 7-14 days after publishing: watch real players, fix bugs, and adjust monetization.
- 2026-08-26 Matt The Method [2:37](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=157): Expect AI to remove repetitive work, not decision-making; spend the time on choosing ideas and feeding the AI requirements.
- 2026-08-26 Matt The Method [4:41](https://youtu.be/1mYF-cWJPmQ?t=281): Keep shipping: speaker's two AI-built games flopped partly because he stopped working on them; consistent updates matter.
- 2026-08-26 VuukStudios [3:25](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=205): Release a playable base version, not a finished game, then build and fix during the ad campaign; updates are easy to push later.
- 2026-08-26 VuukStudios [11:37](https://youtu.be/m7015JSIrpw?t=697): Most games can be built in a few weeks, not months; the speaker says his fastest solo game took 4-5 days.
- 2026-08-26 kra_DeZ [3:24](https://youtu.be/oMI-GhUcfFE?t=204): Don't abandon games; he says Spin or Die dropped and lost algorithm recommendations after he stopped updating it.
- 2026-08-29 RoyalDev [1:22](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=82): Avoid black-box code: understand what AI-generated code does before using it, or you can't debug or maintain it.
- 2026-08-29 RoyalDev [7:30](https://youtu.be/erhgduyGzdM?t=450): Using AI for logic and systems can cut build time dramatically; the speaker made a game in under a week.
- 2026-08-30 Duckable [16:31](https://youtu.be/3XX3Y74hzq0?t=991): Expect motivation to drop after the honeymoon phase; scope creep (map, matchmaking, AI, UI, music) can make a full game feel impossible, so break it into finished milestones.
- 2026-08-30 janisjanis01 [15:41](https://youtu.be/pjN_aBDHAfY?t=941): Before release, verify the luck/RNG system and server-lock logic; the launch had broken luck and a non-expiring server lock.
- 2026-08-31 RoyalDev [0:43](https://youtu.be/7cRCq-sJa_Q?t=43): Follow tutorials with Studio open and code along, then immediately rebuild the same system on your own without the video to cement it.
- 2026-08-31 RoyalDev [0:43](https://youtu.be/7cRCq-sJa_Q?t=43): Practice each service you learn (e.g. TweenService, Instance.new, RemoteEvents) in a scratch baseplate until you can build it unaided.
- 2026-08-31 RoyalDev [2:46](https://youtu.be/7cRCq-sJa_Q?t=166): Expect roughly two months of consistent practice to reach a shippable level of scripting, per the creator's own experience.
- 2026-08-31 kra_DeZ [3:26](https://youtu.be/g6z18oHjbYE?t=206): Keep the first version minimal; creator built a two-baseplate game with near-zero scope and still got traction.
- 2026-08-31 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gameplay-videos-on-home-help-your-games-get-discovered/4842601): Making a Notoriety-style preview video took a few hours, mostly editing; budget little time for it.
- 2026-09-00 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208): Compute functions (Early 2027) run event-driven or scheduled serverless Luau without repurposing a game server as host.
- 2026-09-01 VuukStudios [8:50](https://youtu.be/2Q4NqQikzsY?t=530): Build a playable loop first, release it, run ads, and judge via stats whether to continue or cut the game.
- 2026-09-06 MonarchDev [6:49](https://youtu.be/haYW1hXahOM?t=409): Before full development, build a bare-bones prototype: remove the baseplate and build only the core action to test whether the idea is worth pursuing.
- 2026-09-08 RoyalDev [0:02](https://youtu.be/MZ-nsDEVXH4?t=2): Write a game plan document before building: game name, core loop, progression, systems, builds, UI, animations and monetisation. Saves time and rework later.
- 2026-09-08 RoyalDev [2:05](https://youtu.be/MZ-nsDEVXH4?t=125): Split the planning doc into tabs or sections per area (systems, builds, animations, attributes) for easy reference in Google Drive or Word.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-publishing-easier-our-vision-and-whats-coming/4865780): Simplified publishing flow (Sept 2026): answer one question instead of the full questionnaire to reach 16+ players in most regions; restricted content still needs the full questionnaire.
- 2026-09-11 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-publishing-easier-our-vision-and-whats-coming/4865780): Asset Iteration (Sept 2026) lets you change private assets while the game is in progress without disruptive moderation; plan iteration around it.
- 2026-09-12 Duckable [16:58](https://youtu.be/MBRNAqkDEFA?t=1018): Beginners should learn to code without AI first, then add AI once they have experience.
- 2026-09-12 Duckable [15:08](https://youtu.be/MBRNAqkDEFA?t=908): Build features one prompt at a time and test each before moving on; Roblox Assistant's stepwise output worked when scoped this way.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [16:12](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=972): Expedited review to reach Select sooner now costs 50,000 Robux (cut from 100,000).
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [20:22](https://youtu.be/53dj7AG3foA?t=1222): Studio starts ~40% faster and Team Test launches ~75% quicker; new project window and faster imports arrive early next year.
- 2026-09-17 Roblox [38:46](https://youtu.be/HXZRH-2zurk?t=2326): If the foundation is outgrown, rebuild it. FIFA Super Soccer replaced its whole base 14 months after launch, live with millions of players.
- 2026-09-18 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-roadmap-2026-fall-update/4880208): Roadmap dates are forecasts; features often slip, so do not plan launches solely around a target date.
- 2026-09-23 VuukStudios [9:46](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=586): Keep builds to 1-3 weeks (a month max); if a game drags longer, cut scope. Use templates for models and UI to save time.
- 2026-09-23 VuukStudios [10:27](https://youtu.be/d5YHwCqwgYM?t=627): Learn scripting well enough to structure a game with a proper framework; AI-only code gets buggy, unreadable and crashes servers, he claims.
- 2026-09-26 rasdev [0:55](https://youtu.be/0_dR_081Hq8?t=55): The 99 Nights team built it from a one-week jam into a roughly three-month build (March to June 2025) with three people.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Creators [31:54](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=1914): Soft-release quietly with small ad spend and let players trickle in before any hype; this let them observe growth before scaling.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Creators [48:25](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=2905): Scope small: find the core fun fast in a small project, then expand; they built some hits in about two weeks and typically spend 2–3 months.
- 2026-09-30 Roblox Creators [48:25](https://youtu.be/hohwBQGQO7c?t=2905): Avoid multi-year scope when learning; Roblox trends move fast, so a long project can miss its moment.
- 2026-10-03 MonarchDev [1:21](https://youtu.be/bbCmt98O9MA?t=81): Split the build into ordered objectives (map, pets, animations, menus, monetization, beta, ads) to avoid a low-effort game.
- 2026-10-10 RoyalDev [0:41](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=41): Write a one-page design doc before coding: name, reference games, genre, core loop, progression, onboarding, monetization. Prevents forgetting design decisions later.
- 2026-10-10 RoyalDev [2:44](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=164): List every script system needed (e.g. map generation, ragdoll physics, UI) and every build, UI, VFX and model need, so the team sees what remains.
- 2026-10-10 RoyalDev [3:26](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=206): Write down ideas immediately; good ideas get forgotten within a day or two before development starts.
- 2026-10-10 RoyalDev [4:38](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=278): Break each task into a Trello checklist and tick items off; he says the checking-off gave motivation while hand-coding.
- 2026-10-10 RoyalDev [5:19](https://youtu.be/DLqlgL_7XrY?t=319): Set fixed deadlines on Trello cards and enable notifications; fixed dates make you more likely to complete work.
- 2026-10-10 mats-dept2 [1:13](https://youtu.be/Y5QtYzd95v8?t=73): Cut mechanics that testing shows hurt controls or tempo; the video dropped sliding because it made controls clumsy.
- 2026-10-11 kra_DeZ [4:48](https://youtu.be/dE0njfPNsdY?t=288): Time spent is not a proxy for income. A game made in a few days (Spinner Die) outearned a 3-year passion project.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [4:44](https://youtu.be/3gnK649VNbY?t=284): Use Scene Analysis continuously while building, not just before launch, to catch problems like unexpected memory use early.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [16:25](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=985): When porting a live game, build a proof-of-concept character controller first, then port into the engine, then into the game; expect the whole game to break mid-migration.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [16:57](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1017): Enable deferred signal behavior and next-generation replication before server authority; it also cut Global Anarchy's frame time from ~6-8ms to ~4-5ms.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [21:03](https://youtu.be/9IqNHhYXK0s?t=1263): Server authority can make anti-cheat code redundant; Aftermath's ~1000-line anti-fly script could be removed after adoption.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [18:07](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1087): Before building a significant feature, have an AI interrogate you with grilling questions; a 10-minute, 13-question session surfaced a real design gap.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [20:31](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1231): Share agent skills in the project repository so teammates can reuse them on new inputs, such as turning character sketches into 3D assets.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [23:16](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=1396): Set up commits to auto-publish via an Open Cloud key stored on disk (not in chat) with standing instructions, giving roughly a one-minute prompt-to-phone-playable loop for a non-live game.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [2:21](https://youtu.be/KB5iAzy86Fo?t=141): Use plan mode to capture intent and review the generated plan markdown before the agent builds, to avoid building the wrong thing.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [3:58](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=238): Wrap risky updates in a config and roll them out as an experiment; watch key metrics and roll back with one action if harm thresholds trip.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [15:27](https://youtu.be/bVU69VImiEc?t=927): Prefer building on Roblox's native analytics stack over rebuilding basics; build custom tools only where your game needs them.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [13:49](https://youtu.be/bkRZeTZAvLw?t=829): Build MVPs fast, launch, and iterate on real player data instead of spending a year building in the dark.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [9:10](https://youtu.be/gCp4zzjjJ14?t=550): Tune movement by iterating in playtests rather than editing code repeatedly; CCL makes feel adjustments fast to test.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [28:32](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1712): Iterate in Studio on feel-critical values like enemy hit reaction and attack wind-up; instant iteration speeds this up.
- 2026-10-12 Roblox [30:39](https://youtu.be/vumAuOqSmNA?t=1839): Ship a prototype early and get player feedback; a competitor launched a similar multiplayer idea first while the team sat on theirs.
- 2026-10-13 TheDankDeveloper [0:40](https://youtu.be/4ojmHWn_rxY?t=40): Never try to build a whole game in one prompt; use AI as a tool and build step by step.
- 2026-10-16 MonarchDev [3:51](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=231): Have the AI study a proven reference game before building, and stay close to what already works rather than reinventing it.
- 2026-10-16 MonarchDev [7:37](https://youtu.be/hPWJlFwp5Xs?t=457): Keep the first release simple: start on one platform and avoid VR or console until you have experience.
- 2026-10-20 rasdev [7:16](https://youtu.be/53OBGXktIPY?t=436): Plan thoroughly before building, then ship quickly to ride a trend rather than spending months on development.
- 2026-10-21 VuukStudios [10:51](https://youtu.be/DTJeR2gJSZo?t=651): Choose between quick trend/template releases (high variance, short life) or a core idea built brick by brick over time; the latter lasts for years.
- 2026-10-22 Deconstructor of Fun [44:42](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=2682): Cut scope to what your team and runway can finish at quality; a ballooned scope forced cuts to quality and a studio closure.
- 2026-10-22 Deconstructor of Fun [46:44](https://youtu.be/oU3oz6Ddz7g?t=2804): Start small and reach a playable, green-lit state fast instead of restarting a large project from square one.
- 2026-10-23 TheDankDeveloper [1:21](https://youtu.be/5S_MYGBtmbM?t=81): Ask the AI for improvement suggestions on a prototype, then tell it to implement the good ones in one batch prompt.
- 2026-10-23 TheDankDeveloper [3:23](https://youtu.be/5S_MYGBtmbM?t=203): Pick game ideas that build on a validated core mechanic; the creator chose a lava-rising tower race because the movement system was already fun and simple to build.
- 2026-10-23 TheDankDeveloper [5:26](https://youtu.be/5S_MYGBtmbM?t=326): Build mechanic by mechanic (round loop, HUD, rewards) and playtest after each step rather than one giant prompt.
- 2026-10-23 tef [3:53](https://youtu.be/OfObWnrucRY?t=233): Build in iterative passes: one large prompt, playtest, then send a numbered fix list. Each pass took roughly 2-4 hours in the speaker's run.
- 2026-10-23 kra_DeZ [10:56](https://youtu.be/sB9W5BXjQpk?t=656): Trend-driven hype games often earn quick money then die; plan to move to the next idea rather than expect long-term retention.
- 2026-10-24 Crusherfire [3:12](https://youtu.be/4n7thZAAF-E?t=192): Use AI to prototype several variants of a gameplay system quickly, then keep iterating within the one that feels best, instead of committing to the first idea.
- 2026-10-25 janisjanis01 [5:28](https://youtu.be/A2QD-eT3IG0?t=328): Ship updates with a live event (e.g. admin abuse event) and a visible player-count spike; the update drove CCU from ~100 to 550 peak.
- 2026-10-26 MonarchDev [1:04](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=64): Build order: create the place, then core features first, then layer everything else on top.
- 2026-10-26 MonarchDev [5:20](https://youtu.be/WA4RzzT39Dw?t=320): Don't run multiple AI sessions editing the same code at once; they overwrite each other and cause broken code loops. Use one main session for integration.
- 2026-10-27 ItsZyonic [3:24](https://youtu.be/EulQilcCXAo?t=204): Duplicate a finished lobby map to create the round map instead of building a second one from scratch.
- 2026-10-27 ItsZyonic [3:24](https://youtu.be/EulQilcCXAo?t=204): Expect mobile Studio typing to be slow; budget extra time for scripting when building on a phone.
- 2026-10-27 ItsZyonic [0:00](https://youtu.be/EulQilcCXAo?t=0): Keep scope tiny for a short build: pick one simple core mechanic and cut extras to finish on time.
- 2026-10-27 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-build-2d-games-with-animatedimages/4960734): Import Aseprite .ase files in one click. Frames, named tags (tracks) and frame timing are preserved, avoiding manual ImageRectOffset scripting.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs): Stage and test config changes in Studio before publishing to live players.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/configs): Use SetTestingValue to test a value on one server only; it does not affect other servers or live players.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/collaboration/collaboration-best-practices): Keep a group to-do list that anyone can add tasks to, so every member has something to work on.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/android-tv-pilot): Dedicate a team member to hit technical milestones and attend milestone reviews within the 90-day window.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/weapons-kit): Use the Weapons Kit prefab weapons from the Toolbox Inventory to build competitive combat quickly instead of coding weapons from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/cleanup-and-reset): Check that a winner still exists before reading it (activePlayers[1] may be nil if the player left) and return a fallback string instead of erroring.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-3): Keep the first area scoped to what can be built in about a week, such as a house with a lawn or a simple forest, then expand later.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-3): Sketch the map with simple shapes like circles and squares before opening Studio; detailed drawings aren't needed for pre-production.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-3): Keep a reference sheet of variable names (e.g. Gold or Rubies) so renamed stats stay consistent across scripts.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/finishing-the-project): Once the core project works, expand with more collectible item types and different upgrade types.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/character-bodies): Follow the basic character creation tutorial with Roblox templates before attempting a custom character from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/coding-concept-abstraction): Plan and structure code with abstractions up front; the time investment keeps programs organized and makes later changes (adding items, sales) much faster.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials): Start with the 'Create your first game' tutorial (3D platformer where coins trade for jump power) to learn core Studio concepts before advanced work.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/modeling/reimport): Reimport memory is local to each device; models imported on another device open the Configure dialog, so set the file path there once per machine.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/create-the-map): Write a short game design document first, covering vision and world sketches, to speed up development and make feedback easier to gather.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/auth/oauth2-registration): Skip the general info and redirect URL steps during development; they are only required before submitting for review.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/auth/oauth2-registration): Once an app is approved for public mode, it cannot be reverted to private mode; editing a public app requires resubmitting for review.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges): Only 5 badges per game are free per 24-hour GMT period; each extra badge in that window costs 100 Robux, so plan badge creation in batches.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/publishing/badges): Reorder up to 50 badges at once via drag-and-drop on the Reorder page; new badges appear at the end of the list by default.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping): Keep prototypes fast and narrow: hit the key aspects of a feature being tested, not the entire feature. Scope is what separates prototyping from building.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping): Use paper prototyping for early exploration; it takes minutes to start versus days or weeks for a virtual environment.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/game-design/prototyping): Prototype in Studio once ideas are settled to catch technical infeasibility early, since paper ideas can be impossible to implement.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/adventure-game-series/collect-items): Start with a print statement to confirm Touched fires before writing the harvesting logic.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/feature-packages/changelog): Make example season/mission times relative to current time so testing in Studio works regardless of date; use absolute times in production configs.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-events): Create events as private first; switch to public when ready to promote. Private events don't appear on the experience detail page.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/beginner-landing-page): Download Roblox Studio (free, PC or Mac) to build any Roblox game; a 2-button mouse with scroll wheel is strongly recommended.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/art/accessories/creating-rigid/publishing): Plan for up to 24 hours of moderation after upload before the item can be edited and enabled for sale.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/coding/next-steps): After variables, learn script types/locations, services, properties and attributes, and events before building custom gameplay behavior.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/coding/test-and-save): Publish to Roblox every ten minutes or after making a big change to avoid losing edits if Studio closes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/coding/test-and-save): Save again later via File > Publish or Alt+P (Option+P on Mac) once the game is published.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/building): Complete a small working project end-to-end (build, test, publish) before attempting a full game.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/get-started): Close unused Studio windows and keep only Explorer and Properties open to reduce clutter while working on a task.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/collaboration): Use Live Collaborators > Join to jump to a teammate's location or exact script line instead of searching for it.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/finishing-the-project): Tune variables like GameSettings.matchDuration and intermission length based on playtest feedback rather than guessing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/landing): Build a test map early to validate gameplay and movement before adding polish.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/support/roblox-account-creation): Install Roblox Studio and confirm it runs on your computer before starting any build work.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/first-experience): Build with a small, scoped first project and ship it; the tutorial's target is a playable game with five projectiles, not a full product.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/change-the-script): Make small, single changes and undo the last one if the project breaks.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/collaboration-best-practices): Have group members manage a shared collective to-do list so anyone can pick up a task when idle.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/creating): Start creating in under an hour: sign up, play a few games, install Studio, take the onboarding tour, then build the 'Create your first game' 3D platformer tutorial.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/characters/character-controller-library): Enable CCL via File > Beta Features > AvatarAbilities Character Controller Library, then Avatar Settings > Movement > Abilities. It is opt-in during beta.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/promotion/experience-guidelines): If content is too mature for the target audience, modify the experience to fit, then resubmit; use Class.PolicyService to adjust content per audience.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/getting-started-for-developers-education): Plan for classroom use: review classroom structures, user needs, and student progress assessment in the Planning for Educational Settings article.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-1/create-a-script): Create scripts in ServerScriptService to keep server code in one dedicated folder.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/fundamentals/coding-1/create-a-script): Remove unnecessary default or test code from scripts once it is no longer needed.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/installation-and-setup): Structure a battle royale as one experience with six places: Lobby, Gameplay, and four Queue places (Default, Deathmatch, Team Deathmatch, Free Play).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/battle-royale/installation-and-setup): Publish with File → Publish As… for the first place, then File → Publish (or Overwrite) for others; re-publish after every place ID change.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/coding/get-started): Plan the game content first: write the story or core idea on paper before building, then number placeholders (name1, food1) for each player input.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/landing): Use a 3-button mouse with scroll wheel for navigating 3D space in Roblox Studio.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-create-and-destroy/landing): Practise by building a multiplayer combat map first; the skills transfer to building battle royales or other combat games.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/coding-the-game-loop): Organise logic as ServerStorage module scripts (GameSettings, MatchManager) required by one ServerScriptService GameManager that runs the main loop.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/battle-royale-series/coding-the-game-loop): Start with rough guessed values in GameSettings (e.g. intermission 5s, match 10s, min players 2, transition 5s) and tune them during testing.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/complete-the-challenge): Extend a finished prototype incrementally by adding content, such as new characters or lines, to keep learning.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/incubator): Apply with a strong prototype, or at least a concrete plan to build one. Program support is tied to milestones, so structure work around milestone deliverables.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/creator-programs/jumpstart): Plan to iterate with collaborators: Jumpstart favors teams committed to launching, growing and collaborating/iterating on feedback.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/avatar-generation): Use the returned waitTime from RequestAvatarGenerationSessionAsync to tell players how long until generation is available.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core/building/create-a-project): Start from a Studio template (e.g. Baseplate) to get a working project faster than building from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save): Publish to Roblox every ten minutes or after a big change, since closing Studio without publishing loses all edits.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/animator/test-and-save): Save again later with File > Publish or Alt+P (Option+P on Mac) once the game is already connected to your account.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/roblox-for-the-ap-csp-create-performance-task): Keep visuals simple: use basic block shapes and colors so students spend limited time on core logic rather than decoration.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/roblox-for-the-ap-csp-create-performance-task): Plan needed scripts before coding: list each script, its location, and its function; ensure at least one function simplifies the code.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/roblox-for-the-ap-csp-create-performance-task): Identify cuttable features upfront so scope can be reduced if time runs short.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-story-games/test-and-save): Publish the whole project to Roblox every ten minutes while working, or after any big change, to save progress.

- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/templates): Start from a matching template (e.g. Obby, Racing, FPS, Combat) to get working mechanics and objects instead of building core systems from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/use-case-tutorials/audio/add-voice-chat): Disable each sample script before testing the next configuration, since they conflict when enabled together.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/scripting): Configure Script Editor settings (font, indentation, autocomplete, tooltips) and enable dark mode early to speed up coding.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/version-history): Use Version History as a rollback point: restoring to a known good state creates a new version rather than overwriting history.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house): Get ideas into 3D and playable quickly; use simple parts and Creator Store assets as temporary placeholders so you can playtest within the first few days.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house): Build early versions with an avatar in Studio to check scale of doors, steps and ceilings.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/construct-the-house): Keep an asset storage place with package links so package edits propagate to all places without working in the main game file.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/artist/test-and-save): Publish to Roblox every ten minutes or after a big change to avoid losing edits if Studio closes.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive/foundational-gameplay-systems): Ship debug cheat buttons for mission completion during development and strip debug objects in the release build.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/resources/frequently-asked-questions-education): Roblox Studio requires a PC or Mac; it cannot run on Chromebooks or phones. Use a mouse with a scroll wheel for easier building.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/roblox-developer/roblox-developer-2): When stuck, redo the last few steps first, then ask a peer, before asking the teacher (Ask 3 Before Me).
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/tutorials/curriculums/core): Learn the manual steps at least once to understand how Studio works, then use Assistant to speed up repetitive work.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies): Build reusable systems (quest, collection, event frameworks) to cut future update cost and time.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/get-started/strategies): Build a minimal MVP that demonstrates the core loop, then get it to players early to iterate and avoid late pivots.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/parts/procedural-models): Use parameter-driven generators to iterate non-destructively: change attributes like StepSize instead of rebuilding models by hand.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/studio/avatar-settings): Use a single source of truth for Avatar Settings across teams and place files, since multi-place universes can cause hard-to-debug avatar behaviour.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/build-it-play-it-island-of-move/opening-the-template): Start from the built-in Move It Simulator template, which includes simulator code and a starter world, then customize it rather than building from scratch.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/avatar/in-experience-creation-makeup): Only attempt in-experience makeup if you're comfortable with scripting and Studio; the docs flag it as an advanced workflow.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/developer/beta-testing-experiences): Plan a beta for 7 to 30 days, long enough to measure D1 and D30 retention.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/education/lesson-plans/intro-to-game-and-coding): Separate phases: build first, then code, then polish/playtest, so students don't switch tasks mid-coding.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager): Organize assets into folders early; folders hold up to 20,000 assets each and nest up to 20 levels deep.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager): Use Copy Asset ID to paste IDs directly into AudioPlayer.AssetId or MeshPart.MeshId properties instead of retyping them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager): Use the search query language: quotes for exact phrases, -term to exclude, term? to boost, and asset_id to pin specific assets.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager): Imported assets go through moderation before joining the owning user or group inventory, so import well before you need them.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/projects/assets/manager): Drag-and-drop and the Move dialog don't work across inventories or on search results; navigate to the folder first.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/production/localization): Set the source language first (defaults to account language, one at a time) and then add supported languages before starting localization.
- 2026-10-28 Roblox Creator Docs [page](https://create.roblox.com/docs/resources/the-mystery-of-duvall-drive): Use Packages to reuse common systems and streamline repeated processes across a project.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creators [4:56](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=296): Fix performance issues before announcing; Rivals hid its launch for three days and used Microprofiler and Script Profiler to clear them, then announced and got to ~100 players.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creators [0:00](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=0): Block out maps and gameplay before detailing art; confirm the core loop feels good before spending time on a full map.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creators [0:42](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=42): Stay small and cohesive with a few core people plus outsourced art, music, VFX and anti-cheat for quality control.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox Creators [7:46](https://youtu.be/gy_fAtIkqfo?t=466): Drop a prototype that is not fun and pivot to a different concept rather than polishing a weak idea.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-2d-isometric-and-top-down-games-with-orthographic-camera/4966772): Enable via File > Beta Features > Orthographic Camera, restart Studio if prompted, then set Camera.ProjectionType to Orthographic under Workspace.Camera.
- 2026-10-30 Roblox DevForum [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weekly-recap-october-26-30-2026/4966810): Use Scene Generation (beta, /generate_scene or Generate > Scene) to prototype scenes from images/prompts in minutes, then edit the generated models, textures and terrain.
